最近 30 天论文

量子自对偶爱因斯坦引力与自对偶杨-米尔斯理论的事实

The facts on quantum self-dual Einstein gravity and self-dual Yang-Mills theory

Noah Miller · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：自对偶爱因斯坦引力是四维中一圈精确、紫外与红外有限、微分同胚不变且非幺正的量子引力理论。其仅有的非平凡振幅是一圈全正螺旋度振幅，并与纯爱因斯坦引力相同。本文以教学方式回顾自对偶引力与自对偶杨-米尔斯理论：给出光锥规范下从完整作用量出发的推导、鬼场解耦、Berends-Giele流、树级单负螺旋度振幅消失、天球 Lw_{1+∞} 对称性、perturbiner、双离壳流对一圈全正振幅的计算、维数平移、幺正切割、BDPR 引力公式、一圈颜色排序及协变表述与规范固定。文中还回顾了天球全息项目的部分结果，并简要评论一圈单负螺旋度引力振幅的 all-n 表达式。

English: Self-dual Einstein gravity is a one-loop exact, UV and IR finite, diffeomorphism invariant, non-unitary theory of quantum gravity in four dimensions. The only non-trivial amplitudes are the one-loop-all-plus amplitudes, and they are equal to those of pure Einstein gravity. In these notes we review SDG and SDYM pedagogically. We walk the reader through key calculations, fill in some gaps in the literature, and point out directions for future research. Topics include: derivation of the self-dual actions from the full Einstein gravity/YM actions in lightcone gauge, decoupling of ghosts, Berends-Giele currents, vanishing of tree-level-one-minus amplitudes, celestial $Lw_{1+\infty}$ symmetry, perturbiners, double-off-shell current calculation of one-loop-all-plus SDYM and SDG amplitudes, dimension shifting, unitarity cuts, the BDPR gravity formula, one-loop color ordering, and the covariant formulations of the self-dual theories as well as their their gauge fixing. We also review some selected results from the celestial holography program, and briefly comment on an all-n expression for the one-loop-one-minus gravity amplitudes.

一句话导读：教学性综述自对偶爱因斯坦引力与自对偶杨-米尔斯理论：一圈精确、有限、非幺正，唯一非平凡的一圈全正振幅及其天球全息联系。

相关度：low — 论文属于天球全息/自对偶规范与引力量子振幅，与用户重点关注的凝聚态全息、探针膜、冷原子、p波序参量及经典引力现象等支线没有直接重叠。

为何库珀对存在于 AdS₂：Yukawa-SYK 模型的谱分析

Why Cooper pairs live in AdS2: a spectral analysis of the Yukawa-SYK model

Veronika Carolin Stangier, J\"org Schmalian · hep-th, cond-mat.str-el, cond-mat.supr-con · v1 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：我们为 Yukawa–Sachdev–Ye–Kitaev 模型中库珀配对的几何表述建立谱基础。从大 N 双定域有效作用出发，导出偶频自旋单态库珀通道中的高斯涨落核。按 Maldacena 与 Stanford 的构造，解析确定任意共形权重 h 下的核本征值 k(h)，并将配对涨落分解为相关 de Sitter 空间拉普拉斯算符的连续谱与分立谱扇区。我们证明超导不稳定性及其附近的普适低能涨落完全位于连续散射扇区，而分立模式保持非临界。将核围绕最低连续模展开，得到限制在连续谱子空间上的 dS₂ 克莱因-戈登作用。该子空间正是逆 Radon 变换到 AdS₂ 良定义的空间。因此投影的双定域库珀对场可映射为在 AdS₂ 中传播的标量物质场，而无需对体理论作进一步谱限制。我们的结果为先前全息表述中隐含的投影提供了缺失的微观依据，并澄清了局域体场如何从双定域配对理论中产生。

English: We establish the spectral foundation of the geometric formulation of Cooper pairing in the Yukawa--Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev model. Starting from the large-$N$ bilocal effective action, we derive the Gaussian fluctuation kernel in the even-frequency spin-singlet Cooper channel. Following the construction of Maldacena and Stanford, we determine the kernel eigenvalue $k\left(h\right)$ analytically for arbitrary conformal weight $h$ and resolve the pairing fluctuations into the continuous and discrete sectors of the associated de-Sitter space Laplacian. We show that the superconducting instability and the universal low-energy fluctuations near it reside entirely in the continuous scattering sector, while the discrete modes remain non-critical. Expanding the kernel about the lowest continuum mode yields a Klein--Gordon action on ${\rm dS}_2$, restricted to the continuous spectral subspace. This is precisely the subspace on which the inverse Radon transform to ${\rm AdS}_2$ is well defined. The projected bilocal Cooper-pair field can therefore be mapped onto a scalar matter field propagating in ${\rm AdS}_2$, without requiring any further spectral restriction on the bulk theory. Our results provide the missing microscopic justification for the projection implicit in earlier holographic formulations and clarify how a local bulk field emerges from the bilocal pairing theory.

一句话导读：通过 Yukawa-SYK 模型的配对涨落谱分析，证明库珀对不稳定性可映射到 AdS₂ 中的标量场，为全息超导的几何表述提供微观依据。

相关度：high — 论文直接研究全息超导/库珀配对，属于用户重点标签“全息超流超导”。

全息超流超导

接触结构诱导的辛近半平坦SU(3)结构在有限N热M理论中

Contact Structure-induced Symplectic Nearly Half-Flat $SU(3)$ Structure in Finite-$N$ Thermal ${\cal M}$-Theory

Aalok Misra · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：由arXiv:1306.4339、2004.07259构造的热QCD类理论的对偶M理论，如arXiv:2211.13186所示，涉及一个支撑接触3结构的闭G2结构七维流形M7，这些接触3结构诱导出一个“横向”SU(3)结构。我们证明该诱导结构属于辛近半平坦类：在红外“MQGP极限”下，dω_Φ严格为零，而dΩ_+被压低为O(e^{-N^{1/3}/O(1)})，使得Chiossi-Salamon挠率类中仅W_2^-非零，完成了始于arXiv:1507.02692、2004.07259、2211.13186的MQGP对偶的G结构分类。作为物理应用，对M7上G2结构正三形式微分的十一维Hodge对偶进行Kaluza-Klein约化，并利用G2/S(nearly)HF挠率数据组织，得到一个径向“磁场”B_KK(r)，其模平方精确分解为外部和内部因子。B_KK(r)满足在视界处有正则奇点的Bessel型常微分方程；可归一性和红外正则性选择J2，且上述内部因子的调和性由大N压低恢复。B_KK通过非线性幂律与IIA型味D6膜的磁场相关，为有限温度弦论中产生弱磁场提供了顶下全息机制。

English: The ${\cal M}$-theory dual of thermal QCD-like theories constructed in arXiv:1306.4339, 2004.07259 involves, as shown in arXiv:2211.13186, a closed $G_2$-structure seven-fold $\mathcal{M}_7$ supporting Contact 3-Structures (C3S) that induce a ``transverse'' $SU(3)$-structure. We prove that this induced structure belongs to the symplectic nearly half-flat (S(nearly)HF) class: $d\omega_\Phi=0$ exactly, while $d\Omega_+$ is suppressed as $\mathcal{O}(e^{-N^{1/3}/\mathcal{O}(1)})$ in the infrared "MQGP limit", leaving $W_\Phi^{SU(3)}=W_2^-$ as the sole non-vanishing Chiossi--Salamon torsion class and completing the $G$-structure classification of the MQGP dual initiated in arXiv:1507.02692,2004.07259, 2211.13186. As a physical application, Kaluza--Klein reduction of the eleven-dimensional Hodge dual of the differential of the $G_2$-structure positive three-form on $\mathcal{M}_7$, organised through the $G_2$/S(nearly)HF torsion data, yields a radial ``magnetic field'' $\mathcal{B}_{\rm KK}(r)$ whose squared norm factorises exactly into external and internal factors. $\mathcal{B}_{\rm KK}(r)$ satisfies a Bessel-type ODE with a regular singular point at the horizon; normalisability and infrared regularity select $J_2$, with harmonicity of the aforementioned internal factor recovered from the large-$N$ suppression. $\mathcal{B}_{\rm KK}$ is related to the magnetic field of the type~IIA flavour $D6$-branes via a non-linear power law, furnishing a top-down holographic mechanism for generating weak magnetic fields in finite-temperature string theory.

一句话导读：证明M理论热QCD对偶中接触3结构诱导的横向SU(3)结构为辛近半平坦，并通过KK约化给出径向磁场，为有限温度弦论弱磁场提供顶下全息机制。

相关度：medium — 属于全息QCD/弦论几何分类，与用户重点标签无直接匹配；涉及D6膜和KK约化，但非用户列出的D3-D7/D3-D5探针膜或经典引力。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

微扰论中所有阶的引力康普顿振幅

Gravitational Compton Amplitude to All Orders in Perturbation Theory

Miguel Correia, Giulia Isabella, Anna M. Wolz · hep-th, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：我们展示了在worldline有效场论中，如何高效计算引力波在致密天体上散射的振幅至牛顿常数G的任意阶。我们的方法求解了一个分波振幅的有效波动方程，其中Wilson系数（潮汐Love数）通过短距离边界条件进入。然后我们对分波求和，以获得用椭圆多对数表示的动量空间康普顿振幅。我们复现了最近至O(G^4)的结果，并获得至O(G^7)的新预测，其中Love数首次在O(G^5)贡献。我们在O(G^7)发现了经典引力振幅中的第一个紫外发散，表明纯点粒子描述在广义相对论中不一致。与黑洞微扰论匹配，我们证明静态Love数在壳上消失，并预测了非零的亚领头Schwarzschild黑洞Love数。

English: We show how amplitudes from the scattering of gravitational waves off compact objects in worldline effective field theory can be efficiently computed to arbitrary order in Newton's constant $G$. Our approach solves an effective wave equation for the partial-wave amplitude in which Wilson coefficients (tidal Love numbers) enter through boundary conditions at short distances. We then perform the sum over partial waves to obtain the momentum-space Compton amplitude in terms of elliptic polylogarithms. We reproduce recent results through $\mathcal{O}(G^4)$ and obtain new predictions up to $\mathcal{O}(G^7)$, with Love numbers first contributing at $\mathcal{O}(G^5)$. We find the first ultraviolet divergence in a classical gravitational amplitude at $\mathcal{O}(G^7)$, showing that a pure point-particle description is not consistent in general relativity. Matching to black hole perturbation theory, we show that static Love numbers vanish on-shell and predict subleading non-zero Schwarzschild black hole Love numbers.

一句话导读：通过worldline有效场论计算了引力波在致密天体上散射的振幅至任意G阶，并发现O(G^7)处经典引力振幅的紫外发散。

相关度：high — 论文主要研究经典广义相对论中的引力散射振幅，与用户重点标签'经典引力/广义相对论'高度相关。

经典引力/广义相对论

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

有限资源下的纠缠容量与全息熵

The Capacity of Entanglement and Holographic Entropies at Finite Resources

Ning Bao, Jacob March · hep-th, quant-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：Ryu-Takayanagi 公式将极小曲面面积与边界子区域的 von Neumann 熵等同起来，并对这种等同留下两个开放问题。首先，几何在多大程度上精确决定熵。Fannes-Audenaert 用 Hilbert 空间维度给出回答，该维度在截断移除时发散，即使两个态非常接近。我们将其替换为纠缠容量，即模能量的方差，其平方根随纠缠面积的平方根增长，而维度因子随规则化体积增长。该界限与维度无关且可达，并使得对于任何容量相对于 S_{vN}^2 乘以迹范数较小的扰动，熵的不确定性为次广延。第二个是面积对单一态意味着什么，因为压缩和稀释率只对多副本定义，而几何描述的是单一态。当单个副本鞍点在 α 接近 1 时占主导，固定 α > 1 的每个光滑 Rényi 熵与 S_{vN} 一致到 O(√S_{vN})，光滑 min 和 max 熵也是如此。因此极小曲面一次性固定态的所有单发熵，大中心荷在渐近等分性质中扮演大副本数的角色。作为结果，我们限制了全息态看似偏离全息熵锥的程度，留下估计与认证的开放问题。

English: The Ryu-Takayanagi formula equates the area of a minimal surface with the von Neumann entropy of a boundary subregion, and leaves two things about that identification open. The first is how sharply a geometry fixes an entropy. Fannes-Audenaert answers with a Hilbert-space dimension, which diverges as the cutoff is removed however close the two states are. We replace it with the capacity of entanglement, the variance of the modular energy, whose square root grows like the square root of the entangling area where the dimensional factor grows like the regulated volume. The bound is dimension-free and saturated, and it makes the ambiguity of the entropy subextensive for any perturbation whose capacity is small compared with $S_{vN}^2$ times the trace norm. The second is what the area means for a single state, since compression and dilution rates are defined only for many copies while a geometry describes one. When a single replica saddle dominates near $\alpha = 1$, every smooth R\'{e}nyi entropy at fixed $\alpha > 1$ agrees with $S_{vN}$ to $\textit{O}(\sqrt{S_{vN}})$, as do the smooth min- and max-entropies. The minimal surface therefore fixes every one-shot entropy of the state at once, with large central charge playing the role of large copy number in the asymptotic equipartition property. As a consequence we bound how far outside the holographic entropy cone a holographic state can appear to fall, leaving estimation and certification open.

一句话导读：研究全息纠缠熵在有限资源下的含义，用纠缠容量替代维度因子，并证明单副本鞍点下所有单发熵由极小曲面固定。

相关度：low — 该工作聚焦全息纠缠熵与量子信息，不涉及用户标记的全息超流、QNM、相变、经典引力等具体研究支线。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

测试 dS₃/ICFT₂ 中纠缠伪熵的全息计算

Testing holographic computation of entanglement pseudo-entropy in dS\textsubscript{3}/ICFT\textsubscript{2}

Liang Li · hep-th, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：我们在引力侧建立一个自下而上的模型，用于研究“可能的”de Sitter/接口CFT (dS/ICFT) 对应关系，并使用 Coleman-De Luccia 瞬子。在将暴胀场剧烈变化的区域吸收到一个膜上之后，时空由两个半径不同的 de Sitter 时空通过膜连接而成，这预期对应一个接口 CFT（假设 dS/CFT 猜想成立）。我们的目标是分析现有的全息计算伪熵（即在这样的时空中的极值曲面）的方法，并论证其中一些方法会与接口 CFT 普适性质所预期的结果产生矛盾；同时我们将结果与解析延拓后的 Anti de Sitter/接口CFT 对应关系 (AdS/ICFT) 进行比较。我们还从垂直于接口的子区域的纠缠熵中读取了 $g$-函数。

English: We establish a bottom-up model for studying the `would-be' de-Sitter/interface CFT (dS/ICFT) correspondence on the gravity side, using the Coleman-De Luccia instanton. After absorbing the region where the inflaton changes dramatically by a brane, the spacetime consists of two de Sitter spacetimes with different radii joined by a brane, which is expected to be dual to an interface CFT provided that the dS/CFT conjecture is correct. Our goal is to analyze existing prescriptions for the holographic computation of pseudo-entropy, i.e. the extremal surface in such a spacetime and argue that some of them would cause parodox with the results expected the universal properties of interface CFT and compare our results with that of Anti de Sitter/interface CFT correspondence (AdS/ICFT) after analytic continuation. We also read off the $g$-function from the entanglement entropy associated with a subregion perpendicular to the interface.

一句话导读：基于 Coleman-De Luccia 瞬子建立 dS/ICFT 引力模型，检验极值曲面法计算伪熵的潜在悖论，并读取 g 函数。

相关度：low — 与给定重点标签无直接交集；属于 dS/ICFT 全息纠缠熵的理论研究。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

双标度平面 N=4 SYM 与 Carroll 全息

Doubly-scaled planar ${\cal N} = 4$ SYM \& Carroll Holography

Arjun Bagchi, Prateksh Dhivakar, Alok Laddha, Partha Paul · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：Okuda 和 Penedones (OP) 证明了四维 N=4 超对称 Yang-Mills (SYM) 理论中四点关联函数存在一个双标度极限，其对偶于以 R^{1,4} 为外部运动学的平直空间树级弦振幅（在 R^{1,9} 中）的平坦空间极限。在本文中，我们展示了由此得到的边界关联函数可用于在未来类光无穷远 I^+ 上定义四维 Carroll 共形场论（CFT）。这是通过将 N=4 SYM 理论（在双标度极限下）的关联函数族映射到 I^+ 实现的，I^+ 是所有渐近平坦时空的共形边界。因此，在 N=4 SYM 理论无穷大 't Hooft 耦合极限中涌现出一个 Carroll CFT。我们将 OP 分析扩展到更高点函数，并展示了平坦空间振幅上的 Gram 条件如何对 SYM 关联函数的双标度极限施加约束。然后，我们利用膨胀弦振幅的软因子化定理，为双标度极限下 N=4 SYM 理论的 1/2 BPS 扇区写出递归关系。最后，我们展示了支撑这种双标度极限的基本思想可以用作工具包，通过非引力有效场论的平坦空间振幅的某些积分变换来构建一类 Carroll 关联函数，从而定义 Carroll CFT。

English: Okuda and Penedones (OP) \cite{Okuda:2010ym} showed the existence of a double scaling limit of four point correlators in ${\cal N}=4$ Supersymmetric Yang Mills (SYM) theory in four spacetime dimensions (4$d$) dual to the flat space limit of tree-level string amplitude in $\mathbb{R}^{1,9}$ with external kinematics in $\mathbb{R}^{1,4}$. In this paper, we show that the resulting boundary correlators can be used to define a 4d Carrollian conformal field theory (CFT) on future null infinity (${\I}^{+}$). This is done by mapping the set of correlators in ${\cal N} = 4$ SYM theory (in the double scaling limit) to ${\I}^{+}$, which is the conformal boundary of all asymptotically flat spacetimes. A Carroll CFT thus emerges in the infinite 't Hooft coupling limit of ${\cal N} = 4$ SYM theory. We extend the OP analysis to higher point functions and show how the Gram conditions on flat space amplitude put constraints on the double scaling limit of SYM correlators. We then use the soft factorization theorem for dilatonic string amplitude to write a recursion relation for $\frac{1}{2}$ BPS sector of ${\cal N} = 4$ SYM theory in the double scaling limit. Finally, we show that the essential ideas underlying such a double scaling limit can be used as a tool-kit to build a class of Carrollian correlators, and hence define a Carrollian CFT, via certain integral transforms of flat space amplitudes of non-gravitational effective field theories.

一句话导读：利用 N=4 SYM 四点关联函数的双标度极限定义未来类光无穷远上的 4d Carrollian CFT，并扩展至高点函数与软因子递归关系，为构造 Carroll 关联函数提供通用工具。

相关度：low — 论文研究 Carroll 全息与平直时空关联函数，与用户重点关注的超流超导、相变、QNM、探针膜等方向不直接相关。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

对宇宙学黑洞最大质量的粗略估计

Crudely Estimated Maximum Mass for a Cosmological Black Hole

Don N. Page · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：M. Milosavljevic 和 D. Merritt 的 N 体模拟表明，对于预期近圆轨道的超大质量黑洞双星，在引力波辐射主导之前，其旋进速率使得无量纲轨道相对速度平方 (v/c)^2 几乎随时间线性增加，其速率约等于 210 Gyr 的倒数（约为当前宇宙年龄的 15 倍），且几乎与双星质量无关。这本身不会导致在当前时间发生并合，但当引力辐射的贡献叠加后，可以使质量高达约 1.4×10^14 太阳质量的等质量黑洞双星发生并合，因此可推测这可能是当前宇宙中黑洞质量的一个非常粗略的上限。这也意味着，对于周期大于约 10 万年的引力波，并合的超大质量黑洞双星产生的引力波应变应当减小。

English: $N$-body simulations by M.~Milosavljevic and D.~Merritt \cite{Milosavljevic:2001vi} suggest that for supermassive black hole binaries in their expected nearly circular orbits, before gravitational wave emission dominates, the inspiral rate causes the dimensionless orbital relative velocity squared, $(v/c)^2$, to increase nearly linearly with time at a rate that is approximately the inverse of 210 Gyr (about 15 times the present age of the universe), nearly independently of the mass of the binary. This by itself would not lead to coalescence by the present time, but when it is augmented by gravitational radiation, it can lead to coalescence for equal-mass black hole binaries up to about $1.4\times 10^{14} M_\odot$, which might then be conjectured to be a very crude upper limit to the mass of black holes in our universe at the present time. This also suggests that the gravitational wave strain from coalescing supermassive black hole binaries should decrease for wave periods greater than about 100\,000 years.

一句话导读：基于超大质量黑洞双星并合时标的粗估，给出宇宙中黑洞质量上限约为 1.4×10^14 太阳质量，并预言长周期引力波应变下降。

相关度：medium — 论文主要研究经典引力下超大质量黑洞双星的并合时标与最大质量估计，不涉及全息或量子引力，但属于用户标记的经典引力支线。

经典引力/广义相对论

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

暗物质与中子星物质相互作用对极端和中等质量比旋近的影响

Effects of the interaction of dark matter and neutron-star matter on extreme and intermediate mass-ratio inspirals

Benjamin A. Wade, Julian Heeck, David A. Nichols · gr-qc, astro-ph.HE, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：极端和中等质量比旋近在致密暗物质分布中，暗物质的影响会印在轨道动力学和这些系统发出的引力波上。以往工作表明，空间引力波探测器可以测量暗物质引起的引力波效应，从而为超大质量黑洞周围暗物质的存在提供证据。在早期工作中，暗物质被假设只有引力相互作用（即没有暗物质自湮灭或暗物质与普通重子或轻子物质之间的相互作用）。本文研究在具有中子星次体的双星中引入暗物质与自身或普通物质的相互作用对引力波的影响。我们考虑广泛的暗物质模型，这些模型改变暗物质分布（尖峰或湮灭平台），并允许吸积到次体上，以及中子星在旋近过程中质量增加或保持不变（除动力学摩擦的纯引力效应外）。我们发现这些相互作用和自湮灭的独特引力波特征，在某些情形下可能足够大，空间探测器可以利用这些系统的引力波观测区分它们。

English: Extreme and intermediate mass-ratio inspirals in a dense dark-matter distribution have the effects of the dark matter imprinted on the orbital dynamics of and the emitted gravitational waves from these systems. Prior work has shown that space-based gravitational-wave detectors can measure the dark-matter-induced effects on the gravitational waves, which would give evidence for the presence of dark matter around the massive black hole. In this earlier work, the dark matter has been assumed to have only gravitational interactions (namely, no dark-matter self-annihilation or interactions between dark matter and ordinary baryonic or leptonic matter). In this article, we investigate the gravitational-wave effects of introducing such interactions of dark matter with itself or with ordinary matter in binaries with a neutron-star secondary. We consider broad classes of dark-matter models that change the distribution of dark matter (spikes or annihilation plateaus) and which permit accretion onto the secondary, as well as an increasing or static mass of the neutron star during the inspiral (in addition to the purely gravitational effect of dynamical friction). We find distinctive gravitational-wave signatures of these interactions and of self-annihilation, which in some of the scenarios could be sufficiently large for space-based detectors to distinguish them using gravitational-wave observations of these systems.

一句话导读：研究暗物质与中子星物质相互作用及自湮灭对极端/中等质量比旋近轨道和引力波信号的影响，发现可区分的引力波特征。

相关度：high — 论文研究极端和中等质量比旋近中暗物质相互作用对轨道动力学与引力波信号的影响，属于经典引力/广义相对论中的致密天体与引力波现象，符合用户“经典引力/广义相对论”支线。

全息原理作为量子引力中的信息原理

Holography as an Information Principle in Quantum Gravity

Sebastian De Haro, Enrico Cinti, Aude Corbeel · physics.hist-ph, gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：我们借鉴特胡夫特对全息原理的原创性表述，分析信息在量子引力中的方法论和概念作用。我们认为，在特胡夫特的工作以及后来的发展中，信息作为一种实质性的指导原则发挥作用。我们区分了这一作用的三个方面：第一，关于区域内可存储信息量的全息界作为理论选择标准，约束可行的量子引力理论。第二，全息作为一种理论等价原则：体理论与边界理论必须描述相同的物理内容，尽管特胡夫特更倾向于更基本、低维且潜在决定论的边界描述。第三，信息的分布与编码将特胡夫特的提议与当代关于体态重建、全息量子纠错和ER=EPR的工作联系起来，在这些工作中，涌现的时空结构与纠缠和冗余的模式相关联。基于这三个作用，我们认为纯粹认识论式或香农式的信息概念在此语境中是不充分的；我们转而概述了一种我们称之为最大概念和中间概念的区分，旨在捕捉全息量子引力中信息的方法论和解释作用。因此，我们建议，在全息和量子引力的语境中，对信息作为解释引导原则的作用进行更系统的哲学处理是可取的。

English: We draw on 't Hooft's seminal formulation of the holographic principle to analyse the methodological and conceptual role of information in quantum gravity. We argue that, in 't Hooft's work and in later developments, information functions as a substantive guiding principle. We distinguish three aspects of this role: First, holographic bounds on the amount of information that can be stored in a region function as theory selection criteria that constrain viable quantum gravity theories. Second, holography functions as a principle of theoretical equivalence: the bulk and boundary theories must describe the same physical content, even though 't Hooft privileges a more fundamental, lower-dimensional, and potentially deterministic boundary description. Third, the distribution and encoding of information connect 't Hooft's proposals to contemporary work on bulk reconstruction, holographic quantum error correction, and ER=EPR, where emergent spacetime structure is tied to patterns of entanglement and redundancy. On the basis of these three roles, we argue that purely epistemic or Shannon-style conceptions of information are inadequate in this context: we instead outline a distinction between what we call maximal and intermediate conceptions that aim to capture the methodological and interpretative roles of information in holographic quantum gravity. We therefore suggest that, in the context of holography and quantum gravity, a more systematic philosophical treatment of the role of information as an interpretation-guiding principle would be desirable.

一句话导读：从信息原则角度分析全息原理在量子引力中的方法论与概念角色，提出最大与中间信息概念。

相关度：low — 论文主要从哲学/概念层面讨论全息原理与信息，不涉及具体全息模型或用户重点研究支线，因此相关度低。

Hayward黑洞在EFT修正光子传播下的引力透镜

Gravitational Lensing of Hayward Black Holes with EFT-Corrected Photon Propagation

Takamasa Kanai · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：我们研究了在考虑有效场论（EFT）对光子传播修正的情况下，Hayward规则黑洞是否可以通过强引力透镜与Schwarzschild黑洞在观测上区分。我们推导了由电磁场与时空曲率非最小耦合引起的修正光子传播定律，并分析了Hayward时空中相应的光子轨迹。利用强偏转极限，我们推导了光子球和偏转角对数发散部分的修正。尽管EFT修正参数上很小，但它们的效应在临界传播区域附近可能被增强，那里偏转角表现出对数发散。我们评估了Hayward参数代表性取值下的强偏转观测量，并与Schwarzschild情形进行了比较。我们发现，EFT对光子传播的修正可以在强透镜观测量上留下特征印迹。此外，随着Hayward参数g^3接近其临界值，EFT修正的贡献变得更加显著，表明依赖于曲率的光子传播修正可能在近临界区域变得特别相关。这些结果表明，强引力透镜可能为区分Hayward规则黑洞与Schwarzschild黑洞提供一种手段。

English: We investigate whether a Hayward regular black hole can be observationally distinguished from a Schwarzschild black hole through strong gravitational lensing when effective field theory (EFT) corrections to photon propagation are taken into account. We derive the modified photon propagation law induced by non-minimal couplings between the electromagnetic field and spacetime curvature, and analyze the resulting photon trajectories in the Hayward spacetime. Using the strong deflection limit, we derive the corrections to the photon sphere and the logarithmically divergent part of the deflection angle. Although the EFT corrections are parametrically small, their effects can be enhanced near the critical propagation region, where the deflection angle exhibits a logarithmic divergence. We evaluate the strong-deflection observables for representative values of the Hayward parameter and compare them with the Schwarzschild case. We find that EFT corrections to photon propagation can leave characteristic imprints on strong-lensing observables. Furthermore, the contribution of the EFT corrections becomes more pronounced as the Hayward parameter $g^3$ approaches its critical value, indicating that curvature-dependent corrections to photon propagation can become particularly relevant in the near-critical regime. These results suggest that strong gravitational lensing may provide a means of distinguishing Hayward regular black holes from Schwarzschild black holes.

一句话导读：研究了EFT修正光子传播下Hayward规则黑洞与Schwarzschild黑洞在强引力透镜中的可区分性。

相关度：low — 论文研究经典引力中的强引力透镜和正则黑洞，不涉及全息、量子引力或凝聚态相关主题，与用户重点研究支线无直接关联。

热密QCD物质全息描述的研究进展

Advances in the holographic description of hot and dense QCD matter

Nicolas Kovensky, Andreas Schmitt · hep-ph, hep-th, nucl-th · v1 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：我们回顾并扩展了在规范-引力对偶框架下，关于非零温度和化学势的类QCD相的最新进展。我们聚焦于Witten-Sakai-Sugimoto模型，这是一种基于弦论的自上而下方法，能够统一处理介子、重子和夸克。我们在相图中定位了诸如π介子凝聚、重子物质和夸克物质等相，并讨论了该全息方法的预言以及弱点和局限性。本综述包含的新颖之处之一是首次全息构造了处于BEC与BCS区间之间的π介子相，然而该相被发现是亚稳的。我们还讨论了全息QCD的未来方向，包括在此模型内以及更广泛的背景下。

English: We review and extend recent progress on QCD-like phases at nonzero temperature and chemical potentials within the gauge-gravity duality. We focus on the Witten-Sakai-Sugimoto model, a top-down approach based on string theory which allows for a unified treatment of mesons, baryons and quarks. Phases such as pion condensation, baryonic and quarkyonic matter are located in the phase diagram, and we discuss predictions as well as weaknesses and limitations of this holographic approach. One of the novelties included in this review is the first holographic construction of a pionic phase between BEC and BCS regimes, which however is found to be metastable. We also discuss future directions in holographic QCD, within the model used here and in a wider context.

一句话导读：回顾了Witten-Sakai-Sugimoto模型在热密QCD相图方面的进展，并首次全息构造了BEC-BCS之间的亚稳π介子相。

相关度：high — 论文直接涉及全息QCD相图与π介子凝聚/BEC-BCS过渡，与用户重点关注的全息超流超导和相变密切相关。

BFSS矩阵模型中的高相关函数

Higher-point correlators in the BFSS matrix model

Anna Biggs, Aidan Herderschee · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：我们研究了BFSS矩阵模型中的高相关函数，该模型是IIA型弦论的非共形对偶，我们使用Witten图和振幅程序技术。虽然Witten图比共形全息理论更复杂，但我们发现额外复杂度很小。作为说明，我们计算了领先的三点Witten图，使用区域方法在挤压极限中求值。我们的结果为未来BFSS矩阵模型的蒙特卡洛和量子模拟提供了新目标。

English: We investigate higher-point correlators in the BFSS matrix model, a non-conformal theory dual to type IIA string theory, using Witten diagrams and techniques from the amplitudes program. While the Witten diagrams are more complex than those relevant for conformal holographic theories, we find that the added complexity is minimal. As an illustration, we compute the leading three-point Witten diagram, evaluating it in a squeezed limit using the method of regions. Our results provide new targets for future Monte-Carlo and quantum simulations of the BFSS matrix model.

一句话导读：利用振幅技术与Witten图，在挤压极限下计算BFSS矩阵模型的三点函数，为模拟提供新目标。

相关度：low — 该论文研究BFSS矩阵模型中的全息关联函数，属于AdS/CFT基础字典，但与用户关注的全息超流、相变、QNM等具体支线无直接关联。

扩展BMS反常与平直空间全息

Extended-BMS Anomalies and Flat Space Holography

Laurent Baulieu, Luca Ciambelli, Tom Wetzstein · hep-th, gr-qc, math-ph, math.MP · v2 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：我们几何地确定了一个定义在渐近平直四维时空零边界上的三维扩展BMS场论的BRST对称性。然后我们独立于四维体讨论其鬼数为零和一的上循环。前者确定了一个局域化在零无穷远边界上的拓扑拉格朗日量，后者对相应的反常进行分类。这一分类重现了体理论中已知的eBMS反常，为平直空间全息提供了罕见的非微扰检验。虽然超平移无反常，超旋转具有独立的中心荷。因此，仅当这些中心荷由体理论决定时，该理论才可能与爱因斯坦引力对偶。

English: We geometrically determine the BRST symmetry of a three-dimensional extended BMS field theory defined at the null boundary of asymptotically flat four-dimensional spaces. We then discuss its ghost number zero and one cocycles, independently of the four-dimensional bulk. The former determine a topological Lagrangian localized on the boundary of null infinity, while the latter classify the corresponding anomalies. This classification reproduces the eBMS anomalies known from the bulk, providing a rare non-perturbative test of flat space holography. While supertranslations are anomaly-free, superrotations admit independent central charges. This theory can thus be dual to Einstein gravity only if these central charges are dictated by the bulk.

一句话导读：通过三维扩展BMS场论的BRST反常分类，为平直空间全息提供了非微扰检验。

相关度：low — 该研究属于平直空间全息与BMS对称性，不属于用户列出的重点标签范围，因此与用户当前关注点相关性低。

对QES处方的一个修正

A revision to the QES prescription

Amir A. Khodahami, Azizollah Azizi · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：在本文中，我们提出了对量子极值曲面(QES)处方的修正，该处方在描述黑洞纠缠熵中起关键作用。虽然已有使用replica trick对原始QES处方的推导，但这些推导依赖于鞍点近似，当replica数量趋近于1时会面临困难。我们强调这些困难，并发展了一个纠缠熵的修正公式，该公式包含对多个曲面的加权求和，这与QES处方依赖单一极值曲面形成对比。我们通过证明与黑洞蒸发早期和晚期阶段的预期结果一致来验证我们的公式，确认其与Page曲线所描绘的幺正演化相符。

English: In this paper, we propose a revision to the Quantum Extremal Surface (QES) prescription, which plays a crucial role in describing the entanglement entropy of black holes. While derivations exist for the original QES prescription using the replica trick, they rely on a saddle point approximation that faces challenges when the number of replicas approaches unity. We highlight these challenges and develop a refined formula for the entanglement entropy that incorporates a weighted summation over multiple surfaces, contrasting the QES prescription's reliance on a single extremal surface. We validate our formulation by demonstrating consistency with expected results for both the early and late stages of black hole evaporation, confirming its alignment with the unitary evolution depicted by the Page curve.

一句话导读：提出QES处方的修正：以多曲面加权求和取代单一极值曲面，解决replica数趋近1时鞍点近似的困难，并满足黑洞蒸发早期/晚期及Page曲线幺正演化。

相关度：high — 论文直接讨论量子极值曲面(QES)处方和黑洞蒸发纠缠熵，属于全息量子引力与黑洞信息问题核心，与理论全息高度相关。

桥接世界sheet CFT 与虫洞

Bridging Worldsheet CFTs and Wormholes

Yoav Zigdon · hep-th · v3 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：我提供了多个共形场论(CFT)在世界sheet上的例子，这些CFT描述了弦在目标空间中连接两个不相交渐近流形的虫洞中的传播。世界sheet方法通过纳入喉部尺寸与弦尺度相当的虫洞，超越了超引力框架。通常，强耦合CFT描述了这些弦性虫洞，包括欧几里得虫洞、双锥和爱因斯坦-罗森桥。最后，我将一个包含SU(2)_k × U(1) Wess-Zumino-Witten模型的特定共形流形解释为在封闭宇宙和虫洞之间调节转变。

English: I provide multiple examples of conformal field theories (CFTs) on the worldsheet that describe string propagation in target space wormholes connecting two disjoint asymptotic manifolds. The worldsheet approach goes beyond the framework of supergravity by incorporating wormholes for which the size of the throat is comparable to the string scale. Typically, strongly coupled CFTs describe these stringy wormholes, which include Euclidean wormholes, double cones, and Einstein-Rosen bridges. Finally, I interpret a certain conformal manifold that contains an $\text{SU}(2)_k \times U(1)$ Wess-Zumino-Witten model as mediating a transition between a closed Universe and a wormhole.

一句话导读：通过世界sheet CFT 构造弦尺度虫洞，包括欧几里得虫洞、双锥和爱因斯坦-罗森桥。

相关度：low — 该研究与用户重点标签（如全息超流超导、相变与气泡成核等）无直接关联，主要聚焦于弦理论中的世界sheet CFT 与虫洞。

用于CFT₃的N=1超Grassmann表示及对AdS与宇宙学关联函数的初步探索

The $\mathcal{N}=1$ Super-Grassmannian for CFT$_3$ and a Foray on AdS and Cosmological Correlators

Aswini Bala, Sachin Jain, Dhruva K. S., Adithya A Rao · hep-th, math-ph, math.MP · v2 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：我们为N=1 SCFT₃中的n点函数构造了超Grassmann积分表示。在该形式中，共形不变性、超对称和特殊超共形不变性通过（算子值的）delta函数约束得以显式实现。该框架的一个重要特点是，我们得到分量关联函数之间的简单代数关系，从而使我们能够仅用其中一个分量关联函数来确定任何分量关联函数。特别地，该形式使我们能够从仅由粒子交换贡献的(A)dS₄边界关联函数出发，构造包含接触图的(A)dS₄边界关联函数。我们通过从对应胶微子（gluino）四点函数确定(A)dS₄杨-米尔斯胶子四点函数来说明这一点。此外，我们建立了超空间中的平直空间极限，发现与已有的平直空间结果完全一致。

English: We construct a Super-Grassmannian integral representation for $n-$point functions in $\mathcal{N}=1$ SCFT$_3$. In this formalism, conformal invariance, supersymmetry, and special superconformal invariance are implemented manifestly through (operator-valued) delta function constraints. An important feature of this framework is the fact that we obtain simple algebraic relations among component correlators, which enable us to determine any component correlator in terms of just one of the component correlators. In particular, this formalism enables us to construct (A)dS$_4$ boundary correlators with contact diagrams from those that receive contributions purely from particle exchanges. We illustrate this by determining the (A)dS$_4$ Yang-Mills gluon four-point function from its gluino counterpart. Further, we establish the flat-space limit in super-space, finding a perfect agreement with existing flat-space results.

一句话导读：构造N=1 SCFT₃的超Grassmann积分表示，并利用分量关联函数之间的代数关系，从粒子交换图构造(A)dS₄边界关联函数，且与平直空间极限一致。

相关度：low — 该论文关注N=1超对称CFT3的关联函数构造及AdS/dS边界关联函数，与用户给定的全息凝聚态、QCD、引力等具体研究支线无直接交集，因此相关性较低。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

自举巨引力子关联函数

Bootstrapping Giant Graviton Correlators

Song He, Canxin Shi, Yichao Tang, Congkao Wen · hep-th · v3 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：我们在 N=4 超杨-米尔斯理论的大 N 极限下，为混合重-轻四点关联函数 <GGOO> 发展了自举方法，其中 O 是应力张量多重态中的手征原场，G 是维度为 O(N) 的（对偶）巨引力子算符，包括最大行列式情形。圈积分被展开到一组带标记的 f-图基中——由于巨引力子的维度为 N，必须包含非平面拓扑——系数则由多种自举条件确定，包括尖点与 OPE 极限中的双三角和三角规则、来自超对称局域化的积分关联函数，以及一个十维隐藏对称性；后者还允许推广到包含一般手征原场 O_k 的关联函数。这些输入共同唯一地确定三圈阶的关联函数，并通过了更多非平凡一致性检验。对于最大行列式算符，我们复现了已知的二圈结果，并得到了完整的三圈修正。

English: We develop bootstrap methods for mixed heavy-light four-point correlators $\langle GGOO\rangle$ in $\mathcal N=4$ super-Yang--Mills theory at large $N$, where $O\equiv {\cal O}_2$ is the chiral primary operator in the stress-tensor multiplet and $G$ are (dual) giant graviton operators with dimension of order $N$, including the maximal determinant case. The loop integrand is expanded in a basis of labelled $f$-graphs -- necessarily including non-planar topologies due to the dimension-$N$ nature of the giant gravitons -- and the coefficients are fixed by various bootstrap conditions including double-triangle and triangle rules in the cusp and OPE limits, integrated correlators from supersymmetric localization, and a ten-dimensional hidden symmetry, the latter also allowing extension to correlators involving generic chiral primaries $\mathcal{O}_k$. Together, these inputs uniquely determine the correlator through three loops, passing further non-trivial consistency checks. For the maximal determinant operator, we reproduce the known results through two loops and obtain the full three-loop correction.

一句话导读：通过自举方法结合尖点/OPE 极限、超对称局域化积分关联函数和十维隐藏对称性，唯一确定了 N=4 SYM 中巨引力子-手征原场混合关联函数到三圈。

相关度：low — 该论文主要研究 N=4 SYM 中巨引力子关联函数的自举，属于 AdS/CFT 基础与字典，不涉及用户重点标签中的全息超流超导、p波、畴壁/涡旋、multi-trace、相变、QNM、冷原子、探针膜或经典引力等支线，因此相关性较低。

规范 D=5 矢量多重态视界上的第二旋转 Killing 场，以及变模黑洞环的不可能定理

A second rotational Killing field on gauged $D=5$ vector-multiplet horizons, and a no-go for varying-moduli black rings

Usman Kayani · hep-th, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：我们研究规范 D=5 超引力耦合矢量多重态的超对称近视界几何，在截面 S 的规范旋转 Killing 矢量 \tilde V 非零的分支上。不假设任何旋转对称性，也不涉由 Killing 旋量构造的标架退化集合。在无边界紧致连通 S 上，独立于 \tilde V 的第二旋转 Killing 场总是存在，并且是整个 S 的等距。在模变化处，它是 U_i=∥η_-∥^2(α Z_i-ε_{ijk}Z^ju^k), u_i=Φ P_i-h_i，是视界数据中的多项式，因此处处光滑；模为常数时视界局部齐性。这些结果的唯一进一步假设是超势 Φ=χ V_I X^I 处处非零——比早期文献中假设的标量势非负更弱。两个子分支由 K=Q_{IJ}C^IC^J 分隔，它在最小理论中恰好为零：K≡0 恢复 Grover, Gutowski, Papadopoulos 和 Sabra 的结果，而其他地方 K>0 且 α 要么恒为零要么处处非零。K≡0、P≡0 和 P≠0 每种情况都出现在紧致 S 上。常模返回 Kunduri 和 Lucietti 的局部几何作为结论而非 ansatz。α 不恒为零的变模给出余齐性一 T^2 作用，其轨道空间是闭区间，因此 S 是 S^3、透镜空间或 S^1×S^2；最后一种被两个全局首次积分排除，一个新的是 α∥η_-∥^4，以及已知的常数旋量范数。因此，α≠0 时变模超对称 AdS_5 黑洞环不可能存在；S^1×S^2 窗口仅在常模或 α≡0 时存活。

English: We study supersymmetric near-horizon geometries of gauged $D=5$ supergravity coupled to vector multiplets, on the branch where the canonical rotational Killing vector $\tilde V$ of the cross-section ${\cal S}$ is non-vanishing. No rotational symmetry is assumed, and nothing about the set where the frame built from the Killing spinors degenerates. On a compact connected ${\cal S}$ without boundary a second rotational Killing field, independent of $\tilde V$, always exists and is an isometry of all of ${\cal S}$. Where the moduli vary it is $$ U_i=\parallel\eta_-\parallel^2\big(\alpha Z_i-\epsilon_{ijk}Z^ju^k\big) , \qquad u_i=\Phi P_i-h_i , $$ a polynomial in the horizon data, hence smooth everywhere; where the moduli are constant the horizon is locally homogeneous. The only further hypothesis for these results is that the superpotential $\Phi=\chi V_IX^I$ is nowhere zero --- weaker than the non-negativity of the scalar potential assumed in the earlier literature. The two sub-branches are separated by $K=Q_{IJ}C^IC^J$, which vanishes exactly in the minimal theory: $K\equiv0$ recovers the result of Grover, Gutowski, Papadopoulos and Sabra, while elsewhere $K>0$ and $\alpha$ is either identically zero or nowhere zero. Each of $K\equiv0$, $P\equiv0$ and $P\not\equiv0$ occurs on compact ${\cal S}$. Constant moduli return the local geometries of Kunduri and Lucietti as a conclusion, not an ansatz. Varying moduli with $\alpha\not\equiv0$ give a cohomogeneity-one $T^2$ action whose orbit space is a closed interval, so ${\cal S}$ is $S^3$, a lens space or $S^1\times S^2$; the last is excluded by two global first integrals, a new one, $\alpha\parallel\eta_-\parallel^4$, and the constant spinor norm already known. A varying-moduli supersymmetric $AdS_5$ black ring therefore cannot exist with $\alpha\not\equiv0$; the $S^1\times S^2$ window survives only at constant moduli or at $\alpha\equiv0$.

一句话导读：证明超对称近视界几何中第二旋转 Killing 场存在，并排除变模 AdS5 黑洞环。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论，研究规范超引力中黑洞视界几何与黑洞环分类，与用户经典引力支线相关，但不涉及全息凝聚态、QNM等其他具体支线。

非最小动能耦合引力中反弹宇宙的宇宙学演化、解析参数化及动力学稳定性

Cosmological Evolution, Analytical Parametrization, and Dynamical Stability of a Bouncing Universe in Non-Minimal Kinetic Coupling Gravity

Alireza Amani, A. S. Kubeka, E. Mahichi · gr-qc, hep-th · v5 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：本文在标量场与爱因斯坦张量之间非最小动能耦合理论的框架下研究了反弹宇宙学。在导出修正的Friedmann方程和场方程后，通过应用反弹条件，对方程组进行了数值求解，而无需对尺度因子假设任何解析形式。因此，尺度因子、标量场以及其他宇宙学量的时间演化直接由模型动力学获得。然后，为了给出宇宙演化的显式形式，引入了尺度因子的解析参数化，该参数化以高精度拟合数值解，并通过将参数化得到的Hubble参数与数值解进行比较确认了其有效性。利用这一框架，研究了宇宙演化过程中包括Hubble参数、共动Hubble视界、能量密度、压强和状态方程参数在内的宇宙学参数行为，并显示了从收缩相到膨胀相的平滑过渡且没有奇点出现。接下来，使用自治动力系统和相空间中的临界点分析研究了模型的稳定性。结果表明，所提出的模型除了描述非奇异反弹情景外，还具有稳定的动力学行为，所引入的解析参数化可作为未来解析研究的合适工具。

English: In this paper, the bouncing cosmology is studied in the framework of the non-minimal kinetic coupling theory between the scalar field and the Einstein tensor. After deriving the modified Friedmann equations and the field equation, by applying the Bounce condition, the system of equations is solved numerically without assuming any analytical form for the scale factor. Thus, the time evolution of the scale factor, the scalar field, and other cosmological quantities are obtained directly from the model dynamics. Then, in order to provide an explicit form for the cosmic evolution, an analytical parameterization for the scale factor is introduced, which is fitted to the numerical solution with high accuracy and its validity is confirmed by comparing the Hubble parameter obtained from the parameterization with the numerical solution. Using this framework, the behavior of cosmological parameters including the Hubble parameter, the comoving Hubble horizon, the energy density, the pressure, and the equation of state (EoS) parameter during the evolution of the universe is investigated, and a smooth transition from the contraction phase to the expansion phase without the occurrence of a singularity is shown. Next, the stability of the model is studied using an autonomous dynamical system and critical point analysis in phase space. The results show that the presented model, in addition to describing a non-singular bouncing scenario, has stable dynamic behavior and the introduced analytical parameterization can be used as a suitable tool for future analytical studies.

一句话导读：研究了非最小动能耦合引力中的反弹宇宙学，通过数值求解和解析参数化展示了非奇异的收缩-膨胀过渡，并利用动力系统分析证明了模型的稳定性。

相关度：low — 研究内容为修改引力下的经典宇宙学反弹模型，与全息或量子引力无关，但属于经典引力理论范畴。

Chern-Simons黑洞热力学的Transgression补全

Transgression Completion for Chern-Simons Black Hole Thermodynamics

Rong-Gen Cai, Li Li, Jun-Kun Zhao · gr-qc, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：量子统计关系将热力学自由能与壳上Euclidean作用量等同起来，是黑洞热力学的基石。在这封快报中，我们证明：对于五维Einstein-Maxwell-Chern-Simons理论中的带电磁黑洞，除非作用量以规范不变的方式表述，否则该关系失效。特别是，通过量子统计关系计算得到的自由能同时违反热力学第一定律和流体力学。我们找到了缺失的有限贡献——一个非局域径向积分，其加入恢复了精确的热力学一致性。值得注意的是，这一项并非特设修正：它与局域Chern-Simons代表元结合后构成transgression形式，得到严格规范不变的作用量。因此，正确的作用量不是局域Chern-Simons项本身，而是其transgression补全——这是物理要求，而非单纯的数学修正。同样的机制在螺旋、转动、标量化、高阶导数和更高维黑洞中仍然成立。我们的工作表明，拓扑相互作用可以从根本上改变Euclidean作用量与热力学势之间的关系，并确立规范不变的transgression形式作为Chern-Simons耦合理论中黑洞热力学的正确框架。

English: The quantum statistical relation, which identifies the thermodynamic free energy with the on-shell Euclidean action, is a cornerstone of black hole thermodynamics. In this Letter, we demonstrate that it fails for charged magnetic black holes in five-dimensional Einstein-Maxwell-Chern-Simons theory unless the action is formulated in a gauge-invariant manner. In particular, the free energy computed via the quantum statistical relation violates both the first law of thermodynamics and hydrodynamics. We identify the missing finite contribution---a nonlocal radial integral---whose inclusion restores exact thermodynamic consistency. Remarkably, this term is not an ad hoc correction: combined with the local Chern-Simons representative, it forms the transgression form, yielding a strictly gauge-invariant action. The correct action is therefore not the local Chern-Simons term alone but its transgression completion---a physical requirement rather than a mere mathematical refinement. The same mechanism persists for helical, rotating, scalarized, higher-derivative, and higher-dimensional black holes. Our work establishes that topological interactions can fundamentally alter the relation between the Euclidean action and the thermodynamic potential and identifies the gauge-invariant transgression form as the correct framework for black hole thermodynamics in Chern-Simons-coupled theories.

一句话导读：证明了具有Chern-Simons耦合的黑洞热力学要求使用transgression补全的规范不变作用量，否则量子统计关系会违反热力学第一定律和流体力学。

相关度：medium — 论文聚焦Chern-Simons耦合下的黑洞热力学与规范不变作用量，与用户关心的经典引力/广义相对论相关，但不涉及其重点标签如全息超导或相变。

第四后牛顿阶椭圆轨道的运动常数与基本频率

Constants of motion and fundamental frequencies for elliptic orbits at fourth post-Newtonian order

David Trestini · gr-qc, hep-th · v3 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：对于准椭圆轨道上的无自旋致密双星系统，我获得了第四后牛顿阶上运动常数（能量和角动量）与基本（径向和方位角）频率之间的保守映射，包括瞬时和尾项贡献。该映射用偏心率增强函数表示，并适当重求和以确保任意偏心率下的精度；特别地，我恢复了圆轨道的已知结果。为了获得该映射，局部动力学采用作用-角变量表述。尾项被视为微扰，先进行时间局域化，然后进行Delaunay平均。这两个操作都需要相空间变量的接触变换，我对其进行了明确控制。利用双星黑洞力学的第一定律，我随后获得了第四后牛顿阶偏心轨道的轨道平均红移不变量；在适当考虑尾项贡献后，它与解析自力的后测地线阶结果[arXiv:2203.13832]完全一致。最后，我利用这些结果将[arXiv:0711.0302]和[arXiv:0908.3854]中第三后牛顿阶获得的能量和角动量通量重新用基本频率表达。

English: In the case of nonspinning compact binary systems on quasi-elliptic orbits, I obtain the conservative map between the constants of motion (energy and angular momentum) and the fundamental (radial and azimuthal) frequencies at the fourth post-Newtonian order, including both instantaneous and tail contributions. This map is expressed in terms of an enhancement function of the eccentricity, which is appropriately resummed to ensure accuracy for any eccentricity; in particular, I recover known results for circular orbits. In order to obtain this map, the local dynamics are expressed using an action-angle formulation. The tail term is treated as a perturbation, which is first localized in time, then Delaunay-averaged. Both operations require a contact transformation of the phase-space variables, which I explicitly control. Using the first law of binary black hole mechanics, I then obtain the orbit-averaged redshift invariant for eccentric orbits at fourth post-Newtonian order; when properly accounting for the tail contributions, it perfectly agrees with analytical self-force at postgeodesic order [arXiv:2203.13832]. Finally, I use these results to re-express the fluxes of energy and angular momentum obtained at third post-Newtonian order in [arXiv:0711.0302] and [arXiv:0908.3854] in terms of fundamental frequencies.

一句话导读：在第四后牛顿阶推导了无自旋致密双星椭圆轨道的运动常数-频率映射，并验证了与解析自力的红移不变量一致性。

相关度：low — 论文专注于经典广义相对论中的后牛顿双星动力学，与用户标注的全息超流、QNM等支线无关。

电磁腔中引力波信号的协变本征模重叠形式

Covariant eigenmode overlap formalism for gravitational wave signals in electromagnetic cavities

Jordan Gu\'e, Tom Krokotsch, Gudrid Moortgat-Pick · gr-qc, hep-th · v3 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：我们发展了一种坐标不变的形式，用于描述引力波与广类谐振探测器的机械和电磁相互作用。通过本征模展开，我们求解了具有动态边界条件的引力波修正的电动力学和弹性力学方程。此外，我们以协变方式考虑了阻尼效应和电磁反作用对机械系统的影响。所得耦合系数对使用微波腔的高频引力波实验特别有用，并允许对任意探测器几何进行直接数值实现。

English: We develop a coordinate invariant formalism which describes the mechanical and electromagnetic interaction of gravitational waves (GWs) with a wide class of resonant detectors. We solve the GW-modified equations of electrodynamics and elasticity with dynamic boundary conditions using an eigenmode expansion. Furthermore, we take damping effects and electromagnetic back-action on mechanical systems covariantly into account. The resulting coupling coefficients are particularly useful for high-frequency gravitational wave experiments using microwave cavities and allow a straightforward numerical implementation for arbitrary detector geometries.

一句话导读：发展了一种坐标不变的协变形式，用本征模展开描述引力波与谐振探测器的机电相互作用，适用于高频微波腔引力波探测。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论方向，与用户重点标签中“经典引力/广义相对论”相关，但并非全息或量子引力研究。

LRS 时空中 Dirac 场的协变方法：共面标架的情形

A covariant approach to the Dirac field in LRS space-times: the case of coplanar frames

Stefano Vignolo, Giuseppe De Maria, Sante Carloni, Luca Fabbri · gr-qc, hep-th, math-ph, math.MP · v3 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：我们利用 Dirac 场的极分解将其描述为有效的自旋流体。然后构建了 Dirac 场的 (1+1+2) 协变形式体系，避免了引入标架场和 Clifford 矩阵。在此框架内，我们分析了自引力 Dirac 场能够自洽嵌入 I、II、III 型局部旋转对称 (LRS) 时空的条件。根据 LRS 对称性要求，我们扩展了先前工作，假设 Dirac 场的速度场和自旋矢量场位于由类时和类空共形生成元逐点定义的平面内。我们给出了一些解析和数值解来说明所提出框架的适用性。

English: We employ the polar decomposition of the Dirac field to describe it as an effective spinorial fluid. We then construct a $(1+1+2)$ covariant formalism for the Dirac field that avoids the introduction of tetrad fields and Clifford matrices. Within this framework, we analyze the conditions under which a self-gravitating Dirac field can be consistently embedded in Locally Rotationally Symmetric (LRS) space-times of types I, II, and III. In accordance with the LRS symmetry requirements, we extend a previous work by assuming that the velocity and spin vector fields of the Dirac field lie in the planes defined pointwise by the generators of the time-like and space-like congruences, which underlie the $(1+1+2)$ decomposition. We present some analytical and numerical solutions to illustrate the applicability of the proposed framework.

一句话导读：本文提出 Dirac 场的 (1+1+2) 协变描述，并研究自引力 Dirac 场在 LRS 时空中的一致嵌入条件，给出解析与数值解。

相关度：high — 论文直接研究经典引力中 Dirac 场嵌入 LRS 时空的条件，与用户重点标签“经典引力/广义相对论”完全匹配；不涉及全息对偶或量子引力。

连续引力波定时模型精度验证：LALSuite与PINT的比较

Validating Timing-Model Accuracy for Continuous Gravitational Waves: A Comparison of LALSuite and PINT

Kartikey Sharma, Reinhard Prix, Maria Alessandra Papa, Curt Cutler · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：我们展示了对用于连续引力波的lalsuite定时模型与pint（一个现代高精度脉冲星定时软件包）进行系统验证的结果。精确的定时模型对于追踪信号相位至关重要，进而对于探测和精确表征连续引力波也至关重要。为了量化定时不准确性的影响，我们推导并验证了领先阶关系μ≈(2πf)^2σ_Δτ^2，其中μ是信号功率的分数损失，f是信号频率，σ_Δτ^2是定时误差的方差。然后，我们将lalsuite定时模型的太阳系和双星分量与pint中的相应模型进行比较。对于原始的lalsuite爱因斯坦延迟实现，总不一致由该分量主导，σ_Δτ≈2.3微秒（对应f=1000Hz时μ≈0.02%）。对于较新的爱因斯坦延迟实现，总不一致（一年内）降至σ_Δτ≲31纳秒（或f=1000Hz时μ≲4e-8），且由观测站对Romer延迟的贡献主导，原因是lalsuite使用了近似地球自转模型。我们还使用474个已编目双星脉冲星的轨道参数测试了双星延迟，并验证了lalsuite源时间导数的自洽性。最后，我们推导并讨论了lalsuite的Shapiro延迟，用于穿过太阳内部的信号，这种情况仅与引力波相关。

English: We present results of a systematic validation of the \lalsuite{} timing model for continuous gravitational waves against \pint{}, a modern high-accuracy pulsar-timing package. An accurate timing model is essential for tracking the signal phase, and hence for detecting and accurately characterizing continuous gravitational waves. In order to quantify the impact of timing inaccuracies, we derive and validate the leading-order relation $\mu \approx (2\pi f)^2\stddtau^2$, where $\mu$ is the fractional loss of signal power, $f$ is the signal frequency, and $\stddtau^2$ is the variance of the timing errors. We then compare the solar-system and binary components of the \lalsuite{} timing model against the corresponding models in \pint{}. With the original \lalsuite{} Einstein-delay implementation, the total disagreement is dominated by that component and has $\stddtau\simeq\SI{2.3}{\micro\second}$ (corresponding to $\mu\simeq\SI{0.02}{\percent}$ at $f=\SI{1000}{\hertz}$). With the newer Einstein-delay implementation, the total disagreement (over one year) drops to $\stddtau\lesssim\SI{31}{\nano\second}$ (or $\mu\lesssim\num{4e-8}$ at $f=\SI{1000}{\hertz}$) and is dominated by the observatory contribution to the \Romer{} delay, owing to the approximate Earth-rotation model used by \lalsuite{}. We additionally test binary delays using orbital parameters from \num{474} catalogued binary pulsars and verify the self-consistency of the \lalsuite{} source-time derivatives. Finally, we derive and discuss the \lalsuite{} Shapiro delay for signals passing through the solar interior, a case only relevant to gravitational waves.

一句话导读：验证了LALSuite连续引力波定时模型与PINT的精度，量化了定时误差对信号功率损失的影响。

相关度：medium — 论文关注连续引力波定时模型的验证，属于经典引力/广义相对论相关的引力波数据分析，但并非纯理论或全息研究，故相关度中等。

带主标量毛的黑洞的扰动：时间演化、准正规模与灰体因子

Perturbations of black holes with primary hair: time evolutions, quasinormal modes and greybody factors

Georgios Antoniou · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：在这项工作中，我们研究了在 shift-symmetric beyond-Horndeski 引力中带主标量毛的黑洞的引力扰动。通过关注轴向扇区，我们研究了扰动方程的几何结构，并在物理和有效度量描述中进行了时域演化，以量化不同毛解响应特性的差异。在频域中，我们随后在物理度量公式中探索了 QNM 谱，从而验证并扩展了在有效度量方法中推导的现有近似计算。我们还计算了相应的灰体因子和吸收截面。最后，我们使用有效势的局域变形作为准正规频率和灰体因子可观测量的相对稳定性的诊断。

English: In this work we study gravitational perturbations of black holes carrying primary scalar hair in shift-symmetric beyond-Horndeski gravity. By focusing on the axial sector, we study the geometric structure of the perturbation equations and perform time-domain evolutions both in the physical and in the effective metric descriptions, in order to quantify differences in the response characteristics of solutions with different hair. In the frequency domain, we then explore the QNM spectrum in the physical-metric formulation, thus verifying and extending existing approximate calculations derived in the effective-metric approach. We also compute the corresponding greybody factors and absorption cross-sections. Finally, we use localized deformations of the effective potential as a diagnostic of the relative stability of quasinormal frequencies and greybody-factor observables.

一句话导读：研究 shift-symmetric beyond-Horndeski 引力中带 primary hair 的黑洞扰动，计算 QNM 谱、灰体因子和吸收截面，并分析有效势变形的影响。

相关度：medium — 论文研究经典修改引力中的黑洞扰动、QNM 和灰体因子，与用户重点标签中的“经典引力/广义相对论”和“QNM与非线性演化”直接相关，但不涉及全息或凝聚态方向。

广义相对论中极大叶层的存在性

On the existence of maximal foliations in general relativity

Bing-Long Chen, Ruo-Bin Wu · math.DG, gr-qc, math-ph, math.MP · v1 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：众所周知，Einstein 方程是 Lorentz 度规的张量方程，因此选择合适的规范条件对于求解至关重要。作为一种强有力的规范条件，极大叶层在广义相对论的两项开创性工作中发挥了关键作用：Minkowski 时空的整体稳定性和有界 L^2 曲率猜想。然而，如果初始超曲面不是极大的，通常的椭圆方法在求解 Einstein 方程时构造极大叶层会遇到根本性困难。本文的目的是在满足真空 Einstein 约束方程的任意初始渐近平坦 Cauchy 面附近，在平均曲率适当小的条件下，给出极大叶层的构造。

English: It is well known that the Einstein equations are tensor equations for a Lorentzian metric. Hence, choosing a suitable gauge condition is crucial for solving them. As a powerful gauge condition, maximal foliations have played a pivotal role in two groundbreaking works in general relativity: the global stability of Minkowski spacetime and the bounded L^2 curvature conjecture. Nevertheless, if the initial hypersurface fails to be maximal, the usual elliptic approach for constructing maximal foliations encounters fundamental difficulties when solving the Einstein equations. The purpose of the paper is to provide a construction of maximal foliations around any initial asymptotically flat Cauchy surface satisfying vacuum Einstein constraint equations, under a suitable smallness condition on the mean curvature.

一句话导读：在渐近平坦真空初值附近，于平均曲率小条件下构造极大叶层。

相关度：high — 论文研究经典广义相对论中 Einstein 方程的规范条件与极大叶层构造，直接属于经典引力/广义相对论支线。

旋转、偏心双黑洞动力学在二阶后牛顿阶的解析解

Analytical Solution of Spinning, Eccentric Binary Black Hole Dynamics at the Second Post-Newtonian Order

Tom Colin, Sashwat Tanay, Laura Bernard · gr-qc · v3 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：近年来引力波探测显示出偏心率和自旋进动的特征，凸显了同时对具有这些特征的双黑洞（BBHs）进行建模的需求。过去十五年来，大多数对旋转BBHs及其相应引力波建模的努力都依赖于启发式地从非进动系统中扭曲波形。这种方法基于经验观察而非第一性原理。本文旨在利用广义相对论中的后牛顿（PN）近似，从第一性原理出发对旋转和偏心BBHs的动力学进行建模。在已有1.5PN解的基础上，我们为具有任意自旋和偏心率的BBHs在2PN阶构建了相对分离矢量、个体黑洞自旋矢量和轨道角动量矢量时间演化的解析解。该解并非完全2PN精度，因为自旋解中微小的轨道时标涨落仅具有领先的1.5PN阶精度而非2PN。然而，结果表明我们新的2PN解仍比先前的1.5PN解提高了一个数量级，突显了自旋解中被忽略的次领头阶振荡的次主导性质。

English: Recent gravitational wave (GW) detections showing signatures of eccentricity and spin precession underscore the need to model binary black holes (BBHs) possessing these features simultaneously. Most efforts over the past fifteen years to model spinning BBHs and their corresponding GWs have relied on heuristically twisting waveforms from nonprecessing systems. This approach is based on empirical observations rather than first principles. This article aims to model the dynamics of spinning and eccentric BBHs from a first-principles approach using the post-Newtonian (PN) approximation within general relativity. Building on the already-existing 1.5PN solution, we construct an analytical solution for the time evolution of the relative separation vector, the individual black hole spin vectors, and the orbital angular momentum vector at 2PN order for BBHs with arbitrary spins and eccentricity. Such a solution is not fully 2PN accurate in that the tiny orbital timescale fluctuations in the solutions for the spins are only leading 1.5PN order accurate, instead of 2PN. However, it is shown that our new 2PN solution is still an order of magnitude improvement over the earlier 1.5PN solution, underlining the subdominant nature of the neglected next-to-leading-order oscillations in the spin solutions.

一句话导读：本文给出旋转、偏心双黑洞在2PN阶的解析动力学解，改进既有1.5PN解。

相关度：low — 论文属于经典引力/后牛顿双黑洞动力学，与用户关注的全息/凝聚态/量子引力研究支线无关。

单模引力中由麦克斯韦场支持的正则黑洞弦与(2+1)维黑洞

Regular Black Strings and $(2+1)$ Black Hole in Unimodular Gravity Supported by Maxwell Fields

G. Alencar, V. H. U. Borralho · gr-qc · v3 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：在这项工作中，我们在单模引力中获得了具有动力学宇宙学函数的正则解。这一广义相对论的替代理论通过对时空体积元施加额外条件，将度规体积密度固定为预先给定的非动力学量，在单模坐标下可表示为常数。在此过程中，宇宙学常数并不直接出现在作用量中，而是作为场方程的积分常数出现。利用能量-动量张量的非守恒，我们证明了该积分常数变为函数 Λ(x)=Λ0+δΛ(x)，其中 Λ0 是真空宇宙学常数，δΛ(r) 是由几何和电磁扇区决定的物质诱导宇宙学贡献。从几何函数 H(r) 的定义出发，我们验证了麦克斯韦电动力学作为解源的有效性。我们进一步表明，与各个解相关的质量函数的线性组合也能由麦克斯韦场一致地支撑，这一性质通常不被非线性电动力学所共有。这一结果使我们能够构造更广泛的正则解族并研究其物理性质。特别是，我们分析了所生成解的能量条件、曲率不变量和热力学性质。

English: In this work, we obtain regular solutions with a dynamical cosmological function in unimodular gravity. This alternative theory to General Relativity imposes an additional condition on the spacetime volume element by fixing the metric volume density to a prescribed non-dynamical quantity, which can be represented by a constant in unimodular coordinates. In this procedure, the cosmological constant does not appear directly in the action, but rather as an integration constant of the field equations. By using the non-conservation of the energy-momentum tensor, we show that the integration constant becomes a function $\Lambda(x)=\Lambda_0+\delta\Lambda(x)$, where $\Lambda_0$ is the vacuum cosmological constant and $\delta\Lambda(r)$ is a matter-induced cosmological contribution determined by the geometry and the electromagnetic sector. From the definition of the geometric function $H(r)$, we verify the validity of Maxwell electrodynamics as a source for the solutions. We further show that linear combinations of the mass functions associated with the individual solutions can also be consistently supported by the Maxwell field, a property that is not generally shared by nonlinear electrodynamics. This result allows us to construct a broader family of regular solutions and investigate their physical properties. In particular, we analyze the energy conditions, curvature invariants, and thermodynamic properties of the generated solutions.

一句话导读：在单模引力中构造由麦克斯韦场支持的规则黑洞解，并分析其能量条件、曲率不变量与热力学性质。

相关度：low — 该研究与全息原理/AdS-CFT无关，属于经典引力与修改引力领域。

法布里-珀罗腔中光悬浮传感器的引力波信号与噪声响应

Gravitational wave signal and noise response of an optically levitated sensor in a Fabry-P\'erot cavity

Andrew Laeuger, Shafaq Gulzar Elahi, Shelby Klomp, Jackson Larsen, Jacob Sprague, Zhiyuan Wang, George Winstone, Maddox Wroblewski, Shane L. Larson, Andrew A. Geraci, Nancy Aggarwal · gr-qc, astro-ph.IM · v2 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：法布里-珀罗腔中的光悬浮传感器已被提议用于高频引力波探测，尽管其引力波灵敏度配置表现出反直觉特征。我们提供了引力波与光学腔中悬浮物体相互作用的新的详细广义相对论推导，证明了可观测响应的规范无关性。我们发现应变信号对陷阱位置有强烈的不对称依赖性，当传感器位于输入镜附近时达到最大，并从多个规范角度对其起源进行了深入解释。这项工作的一个关键新结果是这种不对称性对噪声耦合的影响：输入镜位移与应变信号的耦合相对于端镜位移和共模镜运动可以被高度抑制。这些结果阐明了此类传感器与引力波相互作用的物理起源，并为基于光学悬浮的高频引力波探测器确立了关键设计原则。

English: Optically levitated sensors inside a Fabry-P\'erot cavity have been proposed for high-frequency gravitational-wave (GW) detection, though their configuration for gravitational wave sensitivity exhibits counterintuitive features. We provide a new detailed general relativistic derivation of the interaction between a gravitational wave and a levitated object in an optical cavity, demonstrating gauge independence of the observable response. We find a strong asymmetric dependence of the strain signal on trap position, maximized when the sensor is located near the input mirror, and provide an in-depth explanation of its origin from multiple gauge perspectives. A key new result of this work is the consequence of this asymmetry on the noise coupling: the coupling of input-mirror displacements to the strain signal can be highly suppressed relative to that of end-mirror displacements and common-mode mirror motion. These results clarify the physical origin of the gravitational wave interaction with such a sensor and establish crucial design principles for optical levitation based high-frequency GW detectors.

一句话导读：通过广义相对论推导光悬浮传感器在法布里-珀罗腔中的引力波响应，揭示陷阱位置不对称依赖及噪声耦合抑制。

相关度：high — 论文直接研究经典引力波与光学传感器的广义相对论相互作用，属于经典引力/广义相对论和引力波探测领域，与用户关注的经典引力支线高度相关。

基于空间光学时钟网络的引力波天文学

Gravitational-wave astronomy with a space-based optical clock network

Dhruva Ganapathy, Divya Singh, Sammith Singamsetty, Shimon Kolkowitz · gr-qc, astro-ph.IM, physics.atom-ph, physics.ins-det · v2 · 更新 2026-08-28

中文摘要：自十年前首次探测到双黑洞并合系统以来，引力波天文学已成为天体物理学的重要工具。未来的天基观测站，如激光干涉空间天线（LISA），将解锁毫赫兹（mHz）频段，该频段由于地球噪声而完全无法被地面探测器探测。与此同时，提出的基于原子的引力波探测器，特别是基于空间光学时钟和原子干涉仪的探测器，提供了独特且与传统光学干涉仪互补的能力。其高度可调的特性使得在一个从mHz延伸到Hz乃至更高频段的宽频率范围内能够进行灵敏测量。在这项工作中，我们研究了在空间原子钟网络与LISA等探测器协同运行时使用单向多普勒跟踪。我们分析了主要噪声源，并开发了专用的测量协议，这些协议以目标模式运行，利用外部提供的信号模型来在源啁啾时相干地跟踪响应。通过对模拟信号进行初步参数估计，我们展示了这些探测器如何被用于提取双星引力波源的关键天体物理信息。我们进一步表明，同一仪器在搜索模式下保持灵敏度，该模式不假设源的先验信息。

English: Since the first detection of a merging binary black hole system a decade ago, gravitational-wave astronomy has emerged as a powerful tool for astrophysics. Future space-based observatories, such as the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA), will unlock the millihertz (mHz) band, which remains entirely inaccessible to ground-based detectors due to terrestrial noise. In parallel, proposed atom-based gravitational-wave detectors, specifically those based on space-based optical clocks and atom interferometers, offer capabilities that are unique and complementary to traditional optical interferometers. Their highly tunable character enables sensitive measurements across a broad frequency band extending from the mHz up to and possibly even above the Hz regime. In this work, we investigate the use of one-way Doppler tracking in space-based atomic clock networks operating in concert with detectors like LISA. We analyze the dominant noise sources and develop dedicated measurement protocols, which operate in a targeted mode, using an externally supplied signal model to track the response coherently as the source chirps. Performing preliminary parameter estimation on simulated signals, we demonstrate how these detectors could be used to extract critical astrophysical information about binary gravitational-wave sources. We further show that the same instrument retains sensitivity in a search mode, where no prior on the source is assumed.

一句话导读：研究基于空间光学时钟网络通过单向多普勒跟踪探测引力波的方法，并展示其对啁啾信号和搜索模式的灵敏度。

相关度：medium — 论文涉及引力波天文学，属于经典引力/广义相对论范畴，与全息或量子引力无关，但符合用户关注的经典引力标签。

QCD轴子超致密微型光晕的多信使信号

Multi-messenger signal from QCD-Axion Ultracompact Minihalos

Dorian W. P. Amaral, Enrico D. Schiappacasse, Karla Tapia-Rebolledo · hep-ph, astro-ph.CO, astro-ph.HE · v1 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：我们探索了由恒星质量原初黑洞(PBHs)播种并由QCD轴子暗物质组成的超致密微型光晕(UCMHs)的多信使特征。使用二次内落框架，并采用暴胀前Peccei-Quinn场景中的丰度和等曲率条件，我们数值恢复了特征光晕轮廓，并对最内层区域采用保守的常密度核。我们研究了这些致密结构内部中子星-PBH并合产生的引力波和电磁信号。动力学摩擦产生相对于真空双星的引力波失相，对于质量≲10M_sun的原初黑洞，LISA可以观测到。同时，引力聚焦的轴子可以在中子星磁层中共振转化为光子，产生窄带射电信号。对于附近的银河系中子星，Green Bank射电望远镜GBT的预计灵敏度能在μeV质量范围内捕获QCD轴子带。因此，引力波探测UCMH的存在和结构，而射电信号探测其暗物质的粒子本质，二者的联合观测构成对混合暗扇区的独特检验。

English: We explore the multimessenger signatures from ultracompact minihalos (UCMHs) seeded by stellar-mass primordial black holes (PBHs) and composed of QCD-axion dark matter. Using the secondary-infall framework, with relic-abundance and isocurvature conditions from a pre-inflationary Peccei-Quinn scenario, we numerically recover the characteristic halo profile and adopt a conservative constant-density core for the innermost region. We study the gravitational wave and electromagnetic signatures from neutron-star-PBH inspirals inside these dense structures. Dynamical friction produces a gravitational-wave dephasing relative to the vacuum binary, observable with LISA for primordial black holes with masses $\lesssim\! 10M_\odot$. Simultaneously, gravitationally focused axions can resonantly convert into photons in the neutron-star magnetosphere, producing a narrow-band radio signature. For nearby galactic neutron stars, the projected sensitivity of the Green Bank radio telescope GBT captures the QCD-axion band in the $\mu{\rm eV}$ mass range. Gravitational waves therefore probe the presence and structure of the UCMH, while the radio signal probes the particle nature of its dark matter, making their joint observation a distinctive test of a mixed dark sector.

一句话导读：提出通过中子星-原初黑洞并合的引力波失相和轴子-光子共振转化射电信号，联合探测QCD轴子超致密微型光晕。

相关度：medium — 论文涉及中子星-黑洞并合的引力波信号与轴子暗物质，核心引力波部分属于经典引力/广义相对论支线，但对全息/量子引力主线关联较低。

CFT$_3$实现与$\mathcal{L}_{\Lambda}w_{1+\infty}$代数的天球表示论

CFT$_3$ Realizations and Celestial Representation Theory of the $\mathcal{L}_{\Lambda}w_{1+\infty}$ Algebra

Bin Zhu · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：我们研究了$\mathcal{L}_{\Lambda}w_{1+\infty}$代数的CFT$_3$实现和天球表示。自由标量和Dirac理论产生相同的单粒子光射线作用。该作用在$N=\infty$的临界$O(N)$模型中扩展，而调节矩重现了这里研究的有限但大$N$关联函数中的成对代数。在天球硬引力子模中，投影零条件唯一地选择$\Delta=3$，一个闭的提升子代数精确地湮灭$G^+_3$。非零$\Lambda$的二次Casimir在整个硬模中特征值为三，精确的湮灭子和低自旋Ward恒等式为天球关联函数提供兼容的差分-微分方程。

English: We study CFT$_3$ realizations and celestial representations of the $\mathcal{L}_{\Lambda}w_{1+\infty}$ algebra. Free scalar and Dirac theories yield the same one-particle light-ray action. This action extends to the critical $O(N)$ model at $N=\infty$, while regulated moments reproduce the pairwise algebra in the large-but-finite-$N$ correlators studied here. In the celestial hard-graviton module, the projected null conditions select $\Delta=3$ uniquely, and a closed raising subalgebra annihilates $G^+_3$ exactly. The nonzero-$\Lambda$ quadratic Casimir has eigenvalue three throughout the hard module, and the exact annihilators and low-spin Ward identities give compatible differential-difference equations for celestial correlators.

一句话导读：研究$\mathcal{L}_{\Lambda}w_{1+\infty}$代数在CFT$_3$中的实现及其天球表示，特别关注硬引力子模块。

相关度：medium — 论文属于天球全息与表示论，未直接覆盖用户给定的重点标签（如全息超流超导、经典引力等），但属于全息理论分支，故相关性中等。

矩阵理论中的单负引力子振幅

Single-Minus Graviton Amplitudes from Matrix Theory

Alfredo Guevara, Alexandru Lupsasca, Juan Maldacena, Andrew Strominger · hep-th, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：n点单负引力子振幅在嵌入超引力时保持一些超对称的特殊运动学区域中非零。我们在BFSS矩阵理论中计算这些振幅，通过将它们与A. Sen考虑的一个指标问题联系起来。更精确地说，单负振幅从一个计数四维N=4超Yang-Mills库仑分支中BPS态的指标获得。我们通过使用BPS wall-crossing公式计算指标并将其与引力结果匹配，在特定运动学区域明确检查了这一关系。我们进一步利用BPS wall-crossing来表明该指标在任意运动学区域满足一个w_{1+∞}软定理塔，再次与引力匹配。最后，我们证明对于某些运动学，单负振幅可以通过引力子被平面波散射来实现。

English: The $n$-point single-minus graviton amplitudes are nonzero in a special kinematic region that preserves some supersymmetry when embedded in supergravity. We compute these amplitudes within the BFSS matrix theory by relating them to an index problem considered by A. Sen. More precisely, the single-minus amplitudes are obtained from an index that counts BPS states in the Coulomb branch of four-dimensional ${\cal N}=4$ super Yang-Mills. We explicitly check this relation in a specific kinematic region by computing the index using BPS wall-crossing formulae and matching it to the gravity result. We further employ BPS wall-crossing to show that the index satisfies a tower of $w_{1+\infty}$ soft theorems in any kinematic regime, again matching gravity. Finally, we show that for certain kinematics, the single-minus amplitudes can be realized by scattering a graviton off a plane wave.

一句话导读：利用BFSS矩阵理论中的BPS指标计算单负引力子振幅，并验证其满足w_{1+∞}软定理。

相关度：low — 该论文专注于矩阵理论中的量子引力散射振幅，与用户关注的全息超流/超导等凝聚态全息主题无关。

超越强次可加性：沿重整化群流的全息熵不等式

Beyond Strong Subadditivity: Holographic Entropy Inequalities Along Renormalization Group Flows

Ning Bao, Christian Ferko · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：在共同光锥上的强次可加性（SSA）给出了三维 F-定理的 Casini-Huerta 熵证明。我们研究超越 SSA 的全息熵不等式是否也能约束那些每一个中间理论都具有半经典全息描述的重整化群流。对于共同光锥区域的小且不重叠的形变，我们表明一大类平衡全息不等式的二阶响应仅依赖于已经被 SSA 控制的成对关联。一个六方例子表明，完整的有限不等式仍然包含真正的多体信息，因此该信息的消失是二阶展开的局限，而非不等式本身的局限。两种自然的有限构造无法恢复缺失的信息。然而，我们发现超越 SSA 的信息以两种方式存活。一个五方不等式限制了当一个区域扩大时条件关联的增长速率。另外，奇循环不等式的连续极限给出了对纠缠熵角向形状依赖的约束，且洛伦兹对称性将该约束与径向演化联系起来。因此，超越 SSA 的全息熵不等式确实约束了沿 RG 流的纠缠，尽管我们没有得到第二个普适的 F 函数类比物。

English: Strong subadditivity (SSA) on a common light cone gives the Casini-Huerta entropic proof of the three-dimensional $F$-theorem. We ask whether holographic entropy inequalities beyond SSA similarly constrain renormalization group flows for which every intermediate theory admits a semiclassical holographic description. For small, disjoint deformations of a common light-cone region, we show that the second-order response of a broad class of balanced holographic inequalities depends only on pairwise correlations already controlled by SSA. A six-party example shows that the full finite inequality nevertheless contains genuinely multipartite information, so its disappearance is a limitation of the second-order expansion rather than of the inequality itself. Two natural finite constructions do not recover the missing information. We nevertheless find two ways in which information beyond SSA survives. A five-party inequality bounds the rate at which a conditional correlation grows as one region is enlarged. Separately, a continuum limit of the odd-cyclic inequalities gives a constraint on the angular shape dependence of entanglement entropy, and Lorentz symmetry relates this constraint to radial evolution. Thus holographic entropy inequalities beyond SSA do constrain entanglement along RG flows, although we do not obtain a second universal analogue of the $F$-function.

一句话导读：研究超越强次可加性的全息熵不等式对重整化群流的约束，表明它们虽不能提供第二个F函数，但仍能以多种方式限制纠缠演化。

相关度：medium — 论文核心是全息纠缠熵不等式与RG流，属于全息量子信息方向，与用户关注的全息理论高度相关，但不涉及具体超导/凝聚态或经典引力模型。

引力指标的退耦鞍点与 Farey 尾部

Decoupling saddles of the gravitational index and the Farey tail

Davide Cassani, Alejandro Ruip\'erez, Enrico Turetta · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：我们首先构造了具有 $S^1\times\mathbb{R}^4$ 渐近形式的五维引力超对称指标的一般鞍点，携带任意数量的电荷和 Gibbons-Hawking 中心。然后我们将三电荷构型提升到 IIB 超引力，得到渐近于 $S^1\times \mathbb{R}^4\times S^1\times {\rm K}3$ 并携带 D1-D5-P 电荷的解。通过取退耦极限，这些解给出渐近欧几里得 AdS$_3\times S^3\times {\rm K}3$ 的候选鞍点，对应于对偶 CFT$_2$ 椭圆属。我们专注于两中心鞍点，它们涉及三个整数来指定轨道折叠作用。根据这些整数，鞍点可能具有或没有欧几里得视界，并且可能是光滑的或表现出轨道奇点。我们给出了这些两中心鞍点在合适系综中的在壳作用，并建立了与椭圆属 Farey 尾展开的精确匹配。无视界鞍点是先前文献中讨论过的分数谱流解的欧几里得版本，对应于 $({\rm AdS}_3\times S^3)/\mathbb{Z}_k$ 轨道折叠。黑洞鞍点是这些无视界鞍点的模图像。它们将著名的 ${\rm SL}(2,\mathbb{Z})$ BTZ 黑洞族推广到 $\mathbb{Z}_k$ 轨道折叠背景中。我们还考虑了作为五维黑环和黑洞透镜鞍点退耦极限的渐近 AdS$_3\times S^3$ 解，给出了它们的在壳作用，并确认它们对 D1-D5 椭圆属没有贡献。

English: We first construct general saddles of the five-dimensional gravitational supersymmetric index with $S^1\times\mathbb{R}^4$ asymptotics, carrying an arbitrary number of electric charges and Gibbons-Hawking centers. We then uplift the three-charge configurations to type IIB supergravity, obtaining solutions asymptotic to $S^1\times \mathbb{R}^4\times S^1\times {\rm K}3$, carrying D1-D5-P charges. Upon taking a decoupling limit, these solutions yield asymptotically Euclidean AdS$_3\times S^3\times {\rm K}3$ candidate saddles of the dual CFT$_2$ elliptic genus. We focus on the two-center saddles, which involve three integers specifying an orbifold action. Depending on these integers, the saddles may or may not possess a Euclidean horizon, and may be smooth or exhibit orbifold singularities. We give the on-shell action of these two-center saddles in the appropriate ensemble and establish a precise match with the Farey-tail expansion of the elliptic genus. The horizonless saddles are the Euclidean version of fractional spectral-flow solutions previously discussed in the literature, corresponding to $({\rm AdS}_3\times S^3)/\mathbb{Z}_k$ orbifolds. The black hole saddles are modular images of the horizonless ones. They generalize the well-known ${\rm SL}(2,\mathbb{Z})$ family of BTZ black holes by placing it in the background of the $\mathbb{Z}_k$ orbifold. We also consider the asymptotically AdS$_3\times S^3$ solutions arising as decoupling limit of five-dimensional black ring and black lens saddles, provide their on-shell action and confirm that they do not contribute to the D1-D5 elliptic genus.

一句话导读：该文构造了五维引力指标的一般鞍点，并在退耦极限下与 D1-D5 椭圆属的 Farey 尾展开精确匹配，推广了 BTZ 黑洞族。

相关度：low — 该论文主要研究 AdS3/CFT2 中的引力指标鞍点与椭圆属匹配，不涉及用户列出的重点研究支线。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

BTZ黑膜和有限截止AdS₃中的PEE线程与bit-thread

PEE threads and bit-threads in BTZ black brane and finite-cutoff AdS$_3$

Debarshi Basu, Ashish Chandra, Qiang Wen · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：我们为平面BTZ黑膜和有限截止全息发展PEE线程流。精确的测地线距离核定义了源分辨的PEE线程电流，其叠加给出无散度的bit-thread场。在BTZ中，边界-边界和边界-视界扇区再现纠缠轮廓和热视界通量。在有限截止处，两点PEE核是光滑的，并选择宏观流，这些流通常是非测地线的，尽管由测地线基本线程构建。我们将这些PEE选择的流与独立的正常测地线bit-thread流进行比较，并表明它们饱和相同的RT瓶颈，而在边界校准、端点配对和瓶颈外结构上有所不同。所得构造明确了有限截止纠缠的端点组织和全息最大流的非唯一性。

English: We develop PEE thread flows for the planar BTZ black brane and finite-cutoff holography. Exact geodesic-distance kernels define source-resolved PEE thread currents whose superposition gives divergenceless bit-thread fields. In BTZ, boundary-boundary and boundary-horizon sectors reproduce the entanglement contour and thermal horizon flux. At finite cutoff, the two-point PEE kernels are smooth and select macroscopic flows that are generically non-geodesic despite being built from geodesic elementary threads. We compare these PEE-selected flows with independent normal-geodesic bit-thread flows and show that they saturate the same RT bottleneck while differing in boundary calibration, endpoint pairing and off-bottleneck structure. The resulting constructions make explicit both the endpoint organization of finite-cutoff entanglement and the nonuniqueness of holographic max flows.

一句话导读：研究了BTZ黑膜和有限截止AdS₃中的PEE线程流，揭示了全息最大流的非唯一性及有限截止纠缠的端点结构。

相关度：low — 论文研究与用户重点标签（全息超流超导、相变、QNM、D3-D7探针膜等）无直接关联，属于全息纠缠熵与量子信息的基础理论。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

PEE 线程和位线程在 AdS 引力子区域中

PEE threads and bit threads in gravitational subregions of AdS

Debarshi Basu, Qiang Wen · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：受引力子区域的运动学空间描述启发，我们允许部分纠缠熵（PEE）线程不仅从渐近边界产生，也可以从子区域的边界产生。基于这一设置，我们发展了一个框架来构造源自 AdS 体内部表面的 PEE 线程和位线程的构型。我们分析的情形包括带有端世界（EOW）膜的 Poincaré AdS$_3$ 和平面 BTZ 黑洞膜，以及边界多区间的纠缠楔。我们在这些情形中构造了 PEE 线程和位线程的新颖构型，这为 AdS$_3$/BCFT$_2$ 对应的理解以及受表面/状态对应启发的纠缠楔中的全息定义提供了新的视角。我们发现的一个显著特征是，虽然基本 PEE 线程是测地线，但粗粒化位线程电流的积分曲线一般不是测地线。在显式构造中，积分源电流简化为端点距离差势，这使得无散性、范数界和瓶颈饱和在几何上变得透明。我们的结果阐明了边界、膜、视界和 RT 面自由度如何参与全息纠缠，并提供了 PEE、位线程、子区域运动学空间与表面/状态对应之间的具体联系。

English: Motivated by the kinematic-space description of gravitational subregions \cite{Basu:2026hbg}, we allow partial entanglement entropy (PEE) threads to be sourced not only from the asymptotic boundary, but also from boundaries of subregions. Based on this setup, we develop a framework to construct configurations for PEE threads and bit threads sourced from surfaces in the AdS bulk. The situations we have analyzed include the Poincar\'e AdS$_3$ and the planar BTZ black brane with an end-of-the-world (EOW) brane, and the entanglement wedges of boundary multi-intervals. We construct novel configurations of PEE threads and bit threads in these situations, which shed new light in our understanding of the AdS$_3$/BCFT$_2$ correspondence and the holography defined in the entanglement wedge motivated by surface/state correspondence. A notable feature we found is that, although the elementary PEE threads are geodesics, the integral curves of the coarse-grained bit-thread current are generically not geodesics. Across the explicit constructions, the integrated source currents reduce to endpoint distance-difference potentials, which make divergencelessness, the norm bound and bottleneck saturation geometrically transparent. Our results clarify how boundary, brane, horizon and RT-surface degrees of freedom participate in holographic entanglement and provide a concrete link between PEE, bit threads, subregion kinematic space and the surface/state correspondence.

一句话导读：研究 PEE 线程和位线程在 AdS 体中的来源和配置，揭示边界、膜、视界和 RT 面在纠缠中的角色。

相关度：medium — 论文属于全息纠缠与量子信息基础，与用户给出的具体凝聚态/引力支线关联不强，但可作为全息工具参考。

通过ZX演算看全息码

Holographic codes seen through ZX-calculus

Kwok Ho Wan, H. C. W. Price, Qing Yao, Zhenghao Zhong, Ainhoa Zapirain · quant-ph, hep-th · v3 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：我们从ZX演算的角度重新审视五边形全息量子纠错码。通过将底层张量表示为ZX图，我们通过泡利网（Pauli webs）研究码的稳定子结构。此外，我们获得了对其逻辑算子、编码等距、Rényi熵以及黑洞/虫洞玩具模型的图解理解。然后，受五边形全息码ZX图的启发，我们引入了一族由其双曲镶嵌上的ZX图构造的码，并使用置信传播解码器研究其逻辑错误率。最后，我们展示了如何构造时空ZX图，以实现一个容错量子信道，其中每条内部边都是一个受保护的故障位置。

English: We re-visit the pentagon holographic quantum error correcting code from a ZX-calculus perspective. By expressing the underlying tensors as ZX-diagrams, we study the stabiliser structure of the code via Pauli webs. In addition, we obtain a diagrammatic understanding of its logical operators, encoding isometries, R\'enyi entropy and toy models of black holes/wormholes. Then, motivated by the pentagon holographic code's ZX-diagram, we introduce a family of codes constructed from ZX-diagrams on its dual hyperbolic tessellations and study their logical error rates using belief propagation decoders. Finally, we show how to construct spacetime ZX-diagrams that realise a fault-tolerant quantum channel in which every internal edge is a protected fault location.

一句话导读：利用ZX演算重新表述五边形全息码，并构造一族基于双曲ZX图的全息码与容错量子信道。

相关度：low — 用户重点标签集中在全息超流、QNM、相变、探针膜等强关联物质与引力动力学方向；本文为全息量子纠错码/张量网络的量子信息研究，与这些支线不直接相关，因此相关度低。

从非线性狄拉克系统涌现Kastor-Traschen时空

Emergence of Kastor-Traschen spacetime from a nonlinear Dirac system

Daisuke Ida · gr-qc, hep-th, math-ph, math.MP · v1 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：我们在四维时空中建立了一个唯一性定理，表明非线性旋量系统自然地将背景几何约束为Kastor-Traschen时空，该时空描述动态的宇宙多黑洞。通过分析一个非线性场方程，其中狄拉克旋量的超协变导数由固有费米子流源动，我们在两个物理要求下评估其可积性：相位锁定条件强制实数厄米乘积 \overline{\psi}_R \psi_L，以及纯电来源条件。我们表明，该场方程唯一地迫使旋量成为超协变狄拉克算子的本征矢，其特征值被动态识别为de Sitter背景的哈勃常数。这些结果提供了一种新的几何机制，其中宇宙学常数和动态黑洞构型自发地从费米子凝聚中涌现。

English: We establish a uniqueness theorem in four-dimensional spacetime, demonstrating that a nonlinear spinor system naturally constrains the background geometry to the Kastor-Traschen spacetime, which describes dynamical, cosmic multi-black-holes. By analyzing a nonlinear field equation where the super-covariant derivative of a Dirac spinor is sourced by intrinsic fermion currents, we evaluate its integrability under two physical requirements: a phase-locking condition enforcing a real hermitian product $\overline{\psi}_R \psi_L$, and a pure-electric source condition. We show that the field equation uniquely compels the spinor to be an eigenvector of the super-covariant Dirac operator, with the eigenvalue dynamically identified as the Hubble constant of the de Sitter background. These results provide a novel geometric mechanism wherein both the cosmological constant and dynamical black hole configurations spontaneously emerge from fermion condensates.

一句话导读：证明了非线性狄拉克系统唯一约束背景为Kastor-Traschen时空，揭示宇宙学常数与动态黑洞从费米子凝聚涌现的几何机制。

相关度：high — 论文主要研究经典引力中的Kastor-Traschen时空与非线性Dirac场耦合，并涉及黑洞解，符合经典引力/广义相对论研究支线。

介质中的低频引力轫致辐射与记忆

Low-Frequency Gravitational Bremsstrahlung and Memory in a Medium

Diego Blas, Silvia Gasparotto, Hitoshi Murayama, Jan Sch\"utte-Engel, Jann Zosso · hep-ph, gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：来自早期宇宙的引力辐射为超越标准模型物理学提供了有力窗口。本文研究大质量粒子衰变产生的引力辐射的低频尾部。闵可夫斯基时空中的能量谱 dE/dω 在特征截止频率以上呈平坦的轫致辐射/记忆谱，在截止频率以下呈压低的二次频率标度。衰变产物动量变化的典型速率决定截止频率。我们以两种独立但等价的方式计算 dE/dω：利用 Weinberg 软引力子定理和求解运动方程。这进一步建立了 Weinberg 软引力子定理与引力记忆公式之间的联系，并将其推广以纳入末态散射效应。随后我们计算早期宇宙中大量粒子衰变产生的宇宙学随机引力能量谱 h^2Ω_h。我们证明 h^2Ω_h 在截止频率以上随频率线性标度，在以下转为三次标度。我们的发现揭示与简单线性外推相比低频信号受到压低，这对探测前景有影响。结果还揭示了早期宇宙宇宙学随机引力能谱背景下轫致辐射与记忆之间的联系。

English: Gravitational radiation from the early Universe offers a powerful window into physics beyond the Standard Model (SM). In this paper, we investigate the low-frequency tail of gravitational radiation produced by the decay of massive particles. The energy spectrum in Minkowski space-time, $dE/d\omega$, exhibits two distinct regimes: a flat bremsstrahlung/memory spectrum above a characteristic cutoff frequency and a suppressed quadratic frequency scaling below it. The typical rate of momentum change of the decay products determines the cutoff frequency. We calculate $dE/d\omega$ in two independent but equivalent ways: using Weinberg's soft graviton theorem and by solving the equations of motion. This further establishes the connection between Weinberg's soft graviton theorem and the gravitational memory formula, which we generalize to incorporate the effects of final-state scattering. We then compute the cosmological stochastic gravitational energy spectrum $h^2\Omega_{\rm h}$ generated by many particle decays in the early Universe. We demonstrate that $h^2\Omega_{\rm h}$ exhibits linear scaling with frequency above the cutoff frequency, transitioning to cubic scaling below it. Our findings reveal a suppression of the low-frequency signal compared to a naive linear extrapolation, with implications for detection prospects. Our results also reveal the connection between bremsstrahlung and memory in the context of cosmological stochastic gravitational energy spectra from the early Universe.

一句话导读：研究早期宇宙粒子衰变产生的低频引力轫致辐射与引力记忆，并给出随机引力波背景的频谱标度。

相关度：high — 论文主要研究经典引力中的低频引力轫致辐射、记忆效应和随机引力波背景，直接对应重点标签“经典引力/广义相对论”。

经典引力/广义相对论

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

反超局域性与高原型暴胀模型

Anti-Ultralocality and Plateau Models of Inflation

Joshua Shterenberg, David Garfinkle, Anna I. Rosenzweig, David Shlivko, Paul J. Steinhardt · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：反超局域性指的是耦合的爱因斯坦-标量场方程中空间梯度项相对于速度项的增长。它是暴胀开始前减速膨胀的一个特征。之前的数值相对论研究表明，在幂律暴胀子势的模型中，反超局域性阻碍了暴胀的开始。在本文中，我们表明具有高原形暴胀子势的模型——这被认为是产生低于当前观测上限的张标比的最简单方式——尤其容易受到反超局域性效应的影响。原因在于高原的平坦性以及普朗克密度与高原势能之间约10个数量级的能量密度差距。为了研究这个问题，我们开发了一个评估暴胀模型可行性的通用方案，并将其应用于一个高原势，使用先前经过验证的数值相对论代码。我们发现，从一般的初始条件出发，爱因斯坦方程中梯度项相对于非梯度项的增长要么阻止暴胀持续足够的e折数，要么触发量子失控阶段。我们表明，随着暴胀能量尺度的降低，避免这些问题所需的初始条件微调变得更加严重，这不利于降低张标比的常见方法。

English: Anti-ultralocality refers to the growth of spatial gradient terms relative to velocity terms in the coupled Einstein--scalar field equations. It is a characteristic feature of decelerated expansion before the onset of inflation. Previous numerical relativity studies have shown that anti-ultralocality prevents the onset of inflation in models with power-law inflaton potentials. In this paper, we show that models with plateau-shaped inflaton potentials, which are considered to be the simplest way to generate a tensor-to-scalar ratio below current observational upper limits, are especially vulnerable to anti-ultralocality effects. The reasons are the flatness of the plateau and the energy density gap of $\sim 10$ orders of magnitude between the Planck density and the plateau potential energy. To study the problem, we develop a protocol for assessing the viability of inflationary models in general, and we apply it to a plateau potential using a previously validated numerical relativity code. We find that, starting from generic initial conditions, the growth of gradient terms in the Einstein equations relative to non-gradient terms either prevents inflation from lasting for enough $e$-folds or triggers a phase of quantum runaway. We show that the fine-tuning of initial conditions necessary to avoid these issues becomes more severe as the energy scale of inflation is made smaller, disfavoring common approaches for reducing the tensor-to-scalar ratio.

一句话导读：数值相对论研究表明高原势暴胀模型对反超局域性效应特别脆弱，需要严重的初始条件微调，且在低能标下问题加剧。

相关度：low — 该文研究经典暴胀动力学与反超局域性，与用户关注的全息对偶、强关联系统、引力热力学等方向无直接关联。

统一暗区Horndeski理论中的21厘米亮温度

21-cm Brightness Temperature in a Unified-Dark-Sector Horndeski Theory

Bertha Cuadros-Melgar, Thanasis Karakasis, Eleftherios Papantonopoulos, Maria Petronikolou · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：我们研究标量张量理论对21厘米亮温度的影响。我们考虑Horndeski标量张量理论中的一个具体模型，该模型在平移对称广义Galileon理论框架下描述暗物质与暗能量，并得到有效的统一宇宙流体。我们计算该模型的哈勃参数，绘制特定红移参数z范围内21厘米温度的演化曲线，并与ΛCDM范式的结果进行比较。

English: We investigate the impact of scalar-tensor theories on the 21cm brightness temperature. We consider a specific model in Horndeski scalar-tensor theory in which dark matter and dark energy in the framework of shift-symmetric generalized Galileon theories was described and an effective unified cosmic fluid was obtained. We calculate the Hubble parameter of the model and we plot the curve of the evolution of the 21-cm temperature in a specific range of the redshift parameter $z$ and compare it to the curve corresponding to $\Lambda$CDM paradigm.

一句话导读：在平移对称Horndeski统一暗区模型中计算哈勃参数，研究21厘米亮温度演化并与ΛCDM比较。

相关度：medium — 研究内容属于修改引力宇宙学，与用户重点标签中的“经典引力/广义相对论”相关，但并非传统广义相对论时空/黑洞/引力波现象，相关度中等。
