arXiv 摘要 · PDF

Kerr黑洞周围的稳态Dirac凝聚

Stationary Dirac condensates around Kerr black holes

Sen Guo, Peng-Yu Chen, Yi-Han Huang, Xin Li, Yu Liang, Kai Lin, Lin Wen · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：超轻玻色子场可以在旋转黑洞周围形成宏观云，而类似的稳态费米子凝聚的存在受到其固有自旋的严格限制。在这里，我们建立了一个完整的几何和运动学框架来解决Kerr和Kerr-Newman背景上的大质量Dirac场的稳态束缚态。通过将Kerr-Dirac系统映射为全局积分的源径向问题，我们严格分离了由视界因果性所规定的边界约束。角向部分揭示了一个基本的拓扑区别：因为方位角量子数是严格半整数，规则边界分支阻止局域场密度在旋转轴上消失。因此，旋转费米子云本质上形成全局填充的扁球几何，与标量凝聚的特征性空心环状结构形成鲜明对比。关键的是，我们的径向指数分析揭示了同步Dirac毛缺失的确切数学起源。恰好在运动学同步点处，Dirac算子的Frobenius矩阵是非缺陷的，并且完全没有对数发散。如果没有这些可被边界正则性选择性切除的奇异分支，存在的物理负担完全落在因果通量势垒上，该势垒严格使零源振幅平凡化。这种同步否决表明，黑洞支持宏观稳态场的能力不仅受超辐射运动学控制，还受到局域视界因果性与量子自旋统计之间深刻相互作用的影响。

English: Ultralight bosonic fields can form macroscopic clouds around rotating black holes, whereas the existence of analogous stationary fermionic condensates is strictly constrained by their intrinsic spin. Here we establish a complete geometric and kinematic framework to resolve the stationary bound states of massive Dirac fields on Kerr and Kerr-Newman backgrounds. By mapping the Kerr-Dirac system to a globally integrated sourced radial problem, we strictly isolate the boundary constraints dictated by horizon causality. The angular sector reveals a fundamental topological distinction: because the azimuthal quantum number is strictly half-integer, the regular boundary branches prevent the local field density from vanishing on the rotation axis. Consequently, rotating fermionic clouds inherently form globally filled, oblate geometries, in stark contrast to the hollow toroidal structures characteristic of scalar condensates. Crucially, our radial indicial analysis unveils the exact mathematical origin of the absence of synchronized Dirac hair. Precisely at the kinematic synchronization locus, the Frobenius matrix of the Dirac operator is non-defective and entirely devoid of logarithmic divergences. Without these singular branches to be selectively excised by boundary regularity, the physical burden of existence falls entirely onto the causal flux barrier, which strictly trivializes the zero-source amplitude. This synchronization veto demonstrates that a black hole's capacity to support macroscopic stationary fields is governed not merely by superradiant kinematics, but by the profound interplay between local horizon causality and quantum spin statistics.

一句话导读：该文建立了Kerr和Kerr-Newman背景下大质量Dirac场稳态束缚态的完整框架，指出同步Dirac毛缺失源于视界因果性与量子自旋统计的相互作用。

相关度：high — 论文研究Kerr/Kerr-Newman黑洞上的Dirac场稳态凝聚，属于经典引力背景上的场论分析，与用户重点标签'经典引力/广义相对论'直接相关。

从广义熵重建$f(R)$引力：精确拉格朗日量

Reconstructing $f(R)$ gravity from generalized entropies: exact Lagrangians

Ankit Anand, Sahil Devdutt, Kimet Jusufi, Ayan Chatterjee, Emmanuel N. Saridakis · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：广义视界熵被广泛用作Bekenstein-Hawking面积律的理论修正，但通过Wald构造，它们也可能编码底层引力动力学的修正。我们从给定的熵-面积关系重建度规$f(R)$引力，并表明该过程通过所需的面积-曲率映射本质上依赖分支。在最大对称分支上，其中$A=48\pi/R$精确成立，重建简化为单一求积，可以非微扰地进行。我们获得了几个广义熵的闭式拉格朗日量，并表明熵项$aS_{\mathrm{BH}}^q$产生正比于$R^{2-q}$的曲率项。特别地，Kaniadakis熵产生$1/R$修正，而对数熵产生$R^2\ln R$项。我们进一步推导了一个分支无关的判据$\partial_R^2 f=(ds/dR)d(S/s)/ds$，将Dolgov-Kawasaki稳定性直接与熵泛函联系起来，以及在最大对称分支上有$m_{\mathrm{sc}}^2=S'(s)/(3f_{RR})$。与固定质量Schwarzschild-de Sitter分支的比较揭示了不同的重建拉格朗日量和相反的稳定性性质。最后，弱孤立视界boost荷再现了原始广义熵。这些结果在广义视界热力学和修正引力动力学之间建立了直接的非微扰联系。

English: Generalized horizon entropies are widely used as theoretical modifications of the Bekenstein-Hawking area law, but through the Wald construction they may also encode modifications of the underlying gravitational dynamics. We reconstruct metric $f(R)$ gravity from prescribed entropy-area relations and show that the procedure is intrinsically branch dependent through the required area-curvature map. On the maximally symmetric branch, where $A=48\pi/R$ exactly, the reconstruction reduces to a single quadrature and can be performed non-perturbatively. We obtain closed-form Lagrangians for several generalized entropies and show that an entropy term $a~S_{BH}^{q}$ generates a curvature term proportional to $R^{2-q}$. In particular, Kaniadakis entropy produces a $1/R$ correction, while logarithmic entropy generates an $R^2\ln R$ term. We further derive a branch-independent criterion, $\partial_{R}^{2} f=(ds/dR)d(S/s)/ds$, relating Dolgov-Kawasaki stability directly to the entropy functional, together with $m_{\rm sc}^2=S'(s)/(3f_{RR})$ on the maximally symmetric branch. Comparison with the fixed-mass Schwarzschild-de Sitter branch reveals different reconstructed Lagrangians and reversed stability properties. Finally, the weak-isolated-horizon boost charge reproduces the original generalized entropy. These results establish a direct non-perturbative link between generalized horizon thermodynamics and modified gravitational dynamics.

一句话导读：从广义熵重建$f(R)$引力，得到精确拉格朗日量并联系稳定性。

相关度：medium — 论文主要研究经典修正引力（f(R)）与广义熵的关系，属于经典引力/广义相对论方向，与用户重点关注的多个全息与凝聚态支线不直接相关。

反德西特空间中的真空能问题

Vacuum energy problem in anti-de Sitter space

E. V. Arbuzova, A. D. Dolgov · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：我们研究初始具有大负真空能的宇宙的宇宙学演化，该情态对应于反德西特（AdS）时空。这样的宇宙不可避免地收缩并塌缩成奇点。为防止这种塌缩，我们提出一种基于与曲率标量非最小耦合的标量场的真空能补偿动力学机制。数值和解析计算表明，该机制完全补偿了真空能的引力影响，成功地将曲率驱动为零。结果，宇宙避免了奇点，并演化为典型的辐射主导宇宙学的幂律膨胀。

English: We investigate the cosmological evolution of a universe with an initially large negative vacuum energy, corresponding to an anti-de Sitter (AdS) space-time. Such a universe inevitably contracts and collapses into a singularity. To prevent this collapse, we propose a dynamical mechanism of vacuum energy compensation based on a scalar field non-minimally coupled to the curvature scalar. Numerical and analytical calculations demonstrate that the suggested mechanism completely compensates the gravitational impact of the vacuum energy, successfully driving the curvature to zero. As a result, the universe avoids the singularity and evolves into a power-law expansion, typical for a radiation-dominated cosmology.

一句话导读：提出标量场非最小耦合机制补偿负真空能，避免AdS宇宙收缩奇点并实现辐射主导膨胀。

动量空间中的 Carrollian 共形理论

Carrollian Conformal Theories in Momentum Space

Raffaele Marotta, Arvind Shekar, Mritunjay Verma · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：我们考虑涉及标量算符的 Carrollian 共形场论在动量表象中的行为。我们显式求解动量空间 Ward 恒等式，得到动量空间中 2 点和 3 点 Carrollian 共形关联函数的不同分支。这些分支是对称性允许的结构，以 Carrollian 能量中不同的解析结构为特征，并不一定在单一相容理论中同时实现。对于特定共形维数，动量空间三点函数表现出对数行为。这在坐标空间中没有对应，而是源于联系坐标空间与动量空间关联函数的傅里叶变换中的奇点。我们还分析了动量空间中标量算符 CFT 2 点和 3 点函数的 Carrollian 极限。通过对 CFT 关联函数关于光速采取不同标度，我们获得动量空间中 Carrollian 共形关联函数的不同分支。

English: We consider the Carrollian conformal field theories involving scalar operators in the momentum representation. The momentum space Ward identities are explicitly solved to obtain the different branches of 2 and 3 point Carrollian conformal correlators in the momentum space. These branches are symmetry-allowed structures, characterized by different analytic structures in the Carrollian energies, and are not necessarily realized simultaneously in a single consistent theory. For specific values of the conformal dimensions, the three-point functions in momentum space exhibit logarithmic behaviour. This has no analogue in position space and instead originates from singularities in the Fourier transform relating position and momentum space correlators. We also analyze the Carrollian limit of CFT 2 and 3 point functions of scalar operators in momentum space. By taking different scalings of CFT correlators with respect to the speed of light, we obtain different branches of the Carrollian conformal correlators in the momentum space.

一句话导读：系统求解动量空间中 Carrollian CFT 标量算符的 2、3 点关联函数分支，并分析 Carrollian 极限。

相关度：low — 论文属于 Carrollian/平直时空全息方向，与用户明确标注的超流超导、QNM、经典引力、D3-D7 等具体支线无直接重叠。

Born-Infeld电动力学中Reissner-Nordström-AdS背景上的激发全息超导体的临界温度

Critical Temperatures of Excited Holographic Superconductors in Born--Infeld Electrodynamics on a Reissner--Nordstr\"om--AdS Background

Hoang Van Quyet · hep-th · v4 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：我们在带电的Reissner-Nordström-AdS（RN-AdS）黑洞背景上，研究了具有Born-Infeld（BI）电动力学的探针全息超导体的基态（GS）和两个最低激发态（ES1，ES2）。通过扩展热力学压力P=-Λ/8π来区分这些背景几何需要电荷Q：中性的Schwarzschild-AdS度规对任何M,L^2>0只有一个正视界，无法支持SBH/LBH转变。在正确识别的RN-AdS几何上，我们建立了标量/BI规范探针扇区，构造了凝聚开始的线性特征值问题，并用打靶法求解。我们在三个代表压力（P>P_c，P=P_c，P<P_c）和BI参数b的五个值（{0,1,2,3,5} Q^-1）下获得了GS、ES1、ES2的T_c。T_c随P单调增加，并被b抑制，对所有三个态都是如此，激发态更明显（在b=5 Q^-1时，GS约2%，ES1/ES2约10-27%）。更精细的b扫描显示抑制遵循二次律T_c(b)/T_c(0)=1-alpha b^2，其中alpha(GS)~7.4e-4，alpha(ES1)~4.1e-3，alpha(ES2)~1.1e-2，与阈值模式的径向分布一致：基态模式集中在视界附近，而带节点的激发态模式延伸到体内部更远。我们将其呈现为节点敏感性图像而非已证实的机制，并用对体规范场的显式一阶BI修正使其具体化：这种微分耦合以前没有被独立地分离和量化，独立于留给后续研究的反作用问题。这些结果阐明了BI非线性如何与激发态的节点结构相互作用，为反作用的有限温度全息超导体提供了更清晰的线性基线。

English: We study the ground state (GS) and the two lowest excited states (ES1, ES2) of a probe holographic superconductor with Born-Infeld (BI) electrodynamics on a charged Reissner-Nordstrom-AdS (RN-AdS) black hole background. Distinguishing these background geometries by their extended-thermodynamic pressure P=-Lambda/8pi requires the electric charge Q: the neutral Schwarzschild-AdS metric admits only one positive horizon for any M,L^2>0 and cannot support the SBH/LBH transition. Working on the correctly identified RN-AdS geometry, we set up the scalar/BI-gauge probe sector, formulate the linear eigenvalue problem for the onset of condensation, and solve it by a shooting method. We obtain T_c of GS, ES1, ES2 at three representative pressures (P>P_c, P=P_c, P<P_c) and five values of the BI parameter b in {0,1,2,3,5} Q^-1. T_c increases monotonically with P and is suppressed by b for all three states, more for the excited states (~2% for GS versus ~10-27% for ES1/ES2 at b=5 Q^-1). A finer scan in b shows the suppression follows a quadratic law T_c(b)/T_c(0)=1-alpha b^2, with alpha(GS)~7.4e-4, alpha(ES1)~4.1e-3, alpha(ES2)~1.1e-2, consistent with the radial profiles of the threshold modes: the ground-state mode is concentrated near the horizon, while the nodal excited-state modes extend further into the bulk. We present this as a nodal-sensitivity picture rather than a proven mechanism, and make it concrete with an explicit leading-order BI correction to the bulk gauge field: this differential coupling has not previously been isolated and quantified, independently of the back-reacted problem left to a companion study. These results clarify how BI nonlinearity interacts with the nodal structure of excited states, giving a sharper linear baseline for the back-reacted, finite-temperature holographic superconductor.

一句话导读：研究Born-Infeld电动力学下RN-AdS背景中全息超导体激发态的临界温度，发现激发态对BI非线性更敏感。

相关度：high — 论文直接研究全息超导体的临界温度，属于全息超流超导研究支线。

Dirichlet、Neumann、混合与自对偶全息：(自对偶)杨-米尔斯理论

Dirichlet, Neumann, Mixed and self-dual holography: (self-dual) Yang-Mills theory

Evgeny Skvortsov, Richard Van Dongen · hep-th · v3 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：受自对偶理论在AdS/CFT对应中应用的启发，我们研究了自对偶杨-米尔斯理论及其与杨-米尔斯理论和Chalmers-Siegel理论在Dirichlet、Neumann和混合边界条件下的关系。对自对偶杨-米尔斯理论进行了Fefferman-Graham分析以确定其边界CFT数据。我们提出了自对偶全息方案，定义了三维“自对偶CFT”。在Feynman规范和轴向规范下推导了自对偶杨-米尔斯以及具有混合边界条件的杨-米尔斯/Chalmers-Siegel理论的体-体和边界-体传播子。在旋量-螺旋度形式中计算了三点和四点函数，并阐明了各种理论结果之间的关系。还分析了平直极限和结果的规范(不)依赖性。

English: Motivated by applications of self-dual theories to the AdS/CFT correspondence, we study self-dual Yang-Mills theory (SDYM) and its relation to Yang-Mills theory and to Chalmers-Siegel theory with Dirichlet, Neumann, and mixed boundary conditions. A Fefferman-Graham analysis of SDYM is performed to identify its boundary CFT data. We make a proposal for self-dual holography that defines $3d$ ``self-dual CFTs''. The bulk-to-bulk and boundary-to-bulk propagators for SDYM and for Yang-Mills/Chalmers-Siegel theory with mixed boundary conditions are derived in Feynman and axial gauges. Three- and four-point functions are computed in the spinor-helicity formalism, and the relations among the results in the various theories are clarified. The flat limit and the gauge-(in)dependence of the results are analyzed.

O(d,d) 对称引力与有限耦合全息

$O(d,d)$ symmetric gravity and finite coupling holography

Umut G\"ursoy, Pedro Vicente Marto, Edwan Pr\'eau · hep-th, gr-qc · v3 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：我们构造了一个包含无穷曲率修正的引力理论中的渐近AdS5黑膜解。该作用基于O(d,d)对称ansatz，此ansatz被认为描述弦论经典NSNS扇区。我们发现，对于这一类普遍的理论，视界后的奇点不会被曲率修正所解决。然而，趋近奇点的方式通常会被修改，表现为不同的Kasner指数。我们还证明，在存在非平凡dilaton时，这类曲率修正的一种轻微推广可以在小耦合区域动态产生负宇宙学常数。这提供了一种机制，通过该机制，渐近自由可能出现在假想的QCD弦对偶中。

English: We construct asymptotically AdS$_5$ black brane solutions in a theory of gravity with an infinite series of curvature corrections. The action is based on an $O(d,d)$ symmetric ansatz which has been argued to describe the classical NSNS sector of string theories. We find that, for this general class of theories, the singularity behind the horizon is not resolved by the curvature corrections. The approach to the singularity is however generically modified, being characterized by different Kasner exponents. We also show that, in the presence of a non-trivial dilaton, a slight generalization of these types of curvature corrections can generate dynamically a negative cosmological constant in the region of small coupling. This provides a mechanism through which asymptotic freedom could emerge in the hypothetical string dual of QCD.

D1-D5-P 黑弦的指标鞍点及其退耦极限

Index saddle for the D1-D5-P black string and its decoupling limit

Kanhu Kishore Nanda, P Shanmugapriya, Amitabh Virmani · hep-th, gr-qc · v3 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：Boruch、Emparan、Iliesiu 和 Murthy 近期讨论了 5d 黑弦的指标鞍点，表明黑弦鞍点存在退耦极限，可得到复有限温度 BTZ×S^2 鞍点，用以计算对偶 CFT 的指标。本文对 D1-D5-P 黑弦进行类似构造。我们在四维 STU 模型中构造了四电荷指标鞍点，作为非极端四电荷黑洞的 BPS 极限，并展示其具有新形式的吸引机制。随后通过 4D-5D 联系及一串弦对偶性逐级提升到五维和六维，得到 D1-D5-P 黑弦的引力指标鞍点。我们对该指标鞍点取系统性退耦极限，获得计算 D1-D5 CFT 指标的 BTZ×S^3 鞍点。

English: Boruch, Emparan, Iliesiu, and Murthy recently discussed index saddles for 5d black strings, showing that the black string saddle admits a decoupling limit to a complex, finite-temperature BTZ \times S^2 saddle that computes the index of the dual CFT. In this paper, we pursue an analogous construction for the D1-D5-P black string. We construct a four-charge index saddle in the four-dimensional STU model as the BPS limit of the non-extremal four-charge black hole, and show that it exhibits the new form of attraction. We then uplift it successively to five and six dimensions, via the 4D-5D connection and a chain of string dualities, to obtain the gravitational index saddle for the D1-D5-P black string. We take a systematic decoupling limit of this index saddle and obtain the BTZ \times S^3 saddle that computes the index of the D1-D5 CFT.

时间型全息纠缠熵虚部的探针依赖性

Probe Dependence of the Imaginary Part of HTEE

Mohammad Ali-Akbari · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：我们研究了五维各向异性背景中的全息时间型纠缠熵（HTEE），该背景对偶于强耦合各向异性等离子体。利用复杂极值曲面方法，我们在高温、小各向异性极限$aT \ll 1$下解析计算了HTEE。我们考虑了边界类时间隔的两种取向：一种垂直于各向异性方向，一种平行于各向异性方向。我们发现HTEE的虚部不是几何的普适性质，而是依赖于极值曲面相对于各向异性的取向。这表明由紫外对数发散产生的虚部是一个探针依赖的量。我们的结果表明，HTEE的虚部可以作为极值曲面与对偶场论各向异性自由度之间耦合的一个诊断。

English: We investigate the holographic timelike entanglement entropy (HTEE) in a five-dimensional anisotropic background, dual to a strongly coupled anisotropic plasma. Using the complex extremal surface method, we compute the HTEE analytically in the high-temperature, small-anisotropy limit $aT \ll 1$. We consider two different orientations of the boundary timelike interval: one perpendicular to the anisotropy direction and one parallel to it. We find that the imaginary part of the HTEE is not a universal property of the geometry but depends sensitively on the orientation of the extremal surface relative to the anisotropy. This demonstrates that the imaginary part arising from the UV logarithmic divergence is a probe-dependent quantity. Our results suggest that the imaginary part of HTEE can serve as a diagnostic of the coupling between the extremal surface and the anisotropic degrees of freedom of the dual field theory.

一句话导读：研究了在五维各向异性背景中时间型全息纠缠熵虚部对极值曲面取向的依赖性。

全息比特线：弦图式量子信息流视角

Holographic Bit Threads from String-Diagrammatic Quantum Information Flow

Yi-Yu Lin, Song Cheng · hep-th, quant-ph · v2 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：比特线（bit threads）是全息纠缠熵的最小曲面公式的凸对偶，表现为具有非平凡体内轨道的线状结构。其物理图像长期与 Bell 对解释相关联，但对其具体轨迹（尤其是其非唯一性）的意义仍不清楚。我们提出将范畴量子力学中 Coecke 的量子信息流（QIF）概念应用于全息张量网络这一离散全息体内，并证明其轨迹满足比特线的无散度与密度边界条件。从这种以过程为中心的观点看，比特线的非唯一性变得自然：对于给定资源态，实现同一蒸馏任务的不同协议可以给出不同的 QIF 轨迹。我们进一步用 ZX 弦图分析稳定子型全息张量网络，并表明 QIF 轨迹能够探测比纠缠熵更精细的纠缠结构。

English: Bit threads, arising as the convex dual of the minimal-surface formula for holographic entanglement entropy, are line-like structures with nontrivial bulk trajectories. Their physical picture has long been associated with Bell-pair interpretations, yet the meaning of their detailed trajectories, particularly their nonuniqueness, remains unclear. We propose to apply Coecke's notion of quantum information flow (QIF) in protocol-network geometry, developed within categorical quantum mechanics, to a holographic setup, and show that its trajectories within the discrete holographic bulk---the tensor network---obey the divergence-free and density-bound conditions of bit threads. From this process-centered perspective, bit-thread nonuniqueness becomes natural: for a given resource state, different protocols realizing the same distillation task can give rise to different QIF trajectories. We further analyze stabilizer-type holographic tensor networks using ZX string diagrams and show that QIF trajectories can probe finer entanglement structure beyond the level captured by entanglement entropy.

一句话导读：提出用弦图式量子信息流解释全息比特线的轨迹非唯一性，并展示其可探测纠缠熵之外的精细纠缠结构。

相关度：low — 该论文属于全息量子信息与张量网络方向，但未涉及用户标记的超流、相变、探针膜等具体研究支线。

利用LIGO-Virgo O2数据搜索低质量比双星中的亚太阳质量原初黑洞及其对原初黑洞暗物质比例的约束

Search for sub-solar primordial black holes in low mass ratio binaries with LIGO-Virgo O2 data and implications for the primordial black hole dark matter fraction

Khun Sang Phukon, Gregory Baltus, Sarah Caudill, Sebastien Clesse, Antoine Depasse, Maxime Fays, Heather Fong, Shasvath J. Kapadia, Ryan Magee, Andres Jorge Tanasijczuk · astro-ph.CO, gr-qc, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：我们在LIGO-Virgo探测器第二次观测运行（O2）的数据中，搜索了一个亚太阳质量（SSM）黑洞和主成分质量高于约2太阳质量的双黑洞并合。我们的分析将之前LIGO-Virgo合作搜索扩展到由原初黑洞（PBH）宽质量分布所激发的参数空间区域，该分布在约[2-3]太阳质量处存在峰值，这可以由早期宇宙QCD相变期间状态方程的降低而产生。我们发现四个候选事件通过了信噪比阈值为8和误报率阈值为每年2的筛选，尽管没有一个是统计学上显著到足以构成确定探测。假设搜索结果为无效，我们通过模拟引力波信号注入估计搜索的灵敏体积-时间，推导了PBH模型无关的90%置信度并合率上限。我们使用具有早期宇宙热历史印记的代表性宽PBH质量函数，并考虑早期和晚期PBH双星形成通道，对这些观测限制进行解释。对PBH暗物质比例f_PBH的约束取决于所假设的质量函数和并合率规则。在所有考虑中，上限仍高于f_PBH=1，表明LIGO-Virgo的O2数据不足以在这些PBH质量模型和PBH双星并合的假设下给出有意义的约束。

English: We perform a search for binary black hole mergers with one sub-solar mass (SSM) black hole and a primary component above $\sim 2 M_\odot$ in data from the second observing run (O2) of the LIGO-Virgo detectors. Our analysis extends the parameter space explored by previous LIGO-Virgo Collaboration searches for binaries containing SSM components into a region of parameter space motivated by broad mass distributions of primordial black holes (PBHs) exhibiting a peak around $[2-3] M^{}_\odot$, which can arise from the reduction of the equation of state during the QCD phase transition in the early Universe. Four candidate events are found passing a signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) threshold of 8 and a false alarm rate (FAR) threshold of 2 per year, although none are statistically significant enough to constitute a confident detection. Assuming a null result for the search, we derive PBH model-independent 90\% confidence upper limits on the PBH merger rates by estimating the sensitive volume-time of the search using simulated gravitational-wave signal injections. We interpret these observational limits using a representative broad PBH mass function bearing imprints of the thermal history of the early Universe and considering both early and late PBH binary formation channels. The resulting constraints on the PBH dark-matter fraction, $f^{}_{\rm PBH}$, depend on the assumed mass function and merger-rate prescription. For all considerations, the upper limits remain above $f^{}_{\rm PBH}=1$, indicating the O2 data from LIGO-Virgo are not sensitive enough to place meaningful constraints within the assumptions of the PBH mass model and PBH binary mergers.

一句话导读：利用LIGO-Virgo O2数据搜索亚太阳质量原初黑洞，给出并合率上限，但无法约束原初黑洞暗物质比例。

相关度：low — 该论文属于经典引力波天体物理与原初黑洞观测搜索，与用户关注的全息超流、相变、QNM等理论支线无直接关联，故相关性低。

论可积性的形变与破坏

On deforming and breaking integrability

Ysla F. Adans, Marius de Leeuw, Tristan McLoughlin · cond-mat.stat-mech, cond-mat.str-el, hep-th, math-ph, math.MP, nlin.SI · v2 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：在这篇论文中，我们研究了可积模型的最近邻形变。在按形变参数展开后，我们识别出四种可能的形变类型。首先是简单地破坏或保持可积性的形变。然后我们发现两种不同的微妙情况。第一种是只有当形变参数的所有阶都被考虑时，形变才是可积的。这些的一个例子是出现在全息模型中的长程形变。第二种情况是形变在形变参数的某个阶上是微扰可积的，但不能扩展为可积模型。在本文中，我们针对XXZ自旋链进行了计算，并讨论了每种情况的能级统计。我们发现数值证据表明混沌的开始在这每个模型中出现的方式不同。对于微扰可积模型，我们发现混沌出现的形变强度表现出介于强和弱可积性破缺模型之间的体积标度。

English: In this paper we study nearest-neighbour deformations of integrable models. After expanding in the deformation parameter, we identify four possible types of deformations. First there are deformations that simply break or preserve integrability. Then we find two different subtle cases. The first case is where the deformation is only integrable if all orders of the deformation parameter are taken into account. An example of these are the long-range deformations that appear in holographic models. The second case is when the deformation is perturbatively integrable to some order in the deformation parameter but can not be extended to an integrable model. In this paper we work this out for the XXZ spin chain and discuss the level statistics of each of these cases. We find numerical evidence that the onset of chaos occurs differently in each of these models. For the perturbatively integrable models, we find that the deformation strength at which chaos appears demonstrates a volume-scaling intermediate between strong and weak integrability breaking models.

一句话导读：研究可积模型的最近邻形变，识别四种类型，并用XXZ自旋链的能级统计分析混沌的出现。

相关度：low — 与用户重点标签方向（如全息超导、量子引力等）不直接相关，仅引用了全息模型中的长程形变作为例子。

全息 QCD 中的 θ 角与轴反常

$\theta$ Angle and Axial Anomaly in Holographic QCD

Csaba Cs\'aki, Eric Kuflik, Wei Xue, Taewook Youn · hep-ph, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：我们提出一个自下而上的五维全息描述 QCD θ 真空和 U(1)_A 轴反常。多分支的 θ 真空结构从高维规范场几何地涌现，轴反常通过与 Chern-Simons 项对偶的 Stückelberg 耦合实现。在该框架中，η′ 介子作为体涨落的零模出现，其质量来自反常诱导的 Stückelberg 项。该构造提供了对 η′ 质量反常贡献的透明全息推导，并自然给出了 η′ 质量与 Yang-Mills 拓扑磁化率之间的 Witten-Veneziano 关系。

English: We present a bottom-up holographic description of the QCD $\theta$-vacuum and the $U(1)_A$ anomaly in five dimensions. The multi-branched $\theta$-vacuum structure emerges geometrically from a higher-dimensional gauge field, while the axial anomaly is realized through a St\"uckelberg coupling that is dual to a Chern-Simons term. In this framework, the $\eta'$ meson appears as a zero mode of bulk fluctuations, and its mass arises from the anomaly-induced St\"uckelberg term. The construction provides a transparent holographic derivation of the anomaly contribution to the $\eta'$ mass and naturally reproduces the Witten-Veneziano relation between the $\eta'$ mass and the Yang-Mills topological susceptibility.

一句话导读：自下而上全息 QCD 构造 θ 真空与轴反常，推导 η′ 质量及 Witten-Veneziano 关系。

有限距离处的大黑洞：准局域限制相空间形式

Large black holes at a finite distance: Quasi-local restricted phase space formalism

Bai-Hao Huang, Liu Zhao · gr-qc, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：我们将爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦理论中球对称黑洞解的限制相空间形式推广到准局域情形，即静态观察者位于有限径向距离处。我们证明了RN和RN-AdS黑洞的第一定律和欧拉关系成立，但必须额外包含一对热力学变量，即观察者所在余维2超曲面上的压强P和面积A。对RN黑洞，我们详细分析了其准局域行为。结果表明，准局域描述中的RN黑洞与其渐近描述中的自身行为显著不同，但与渐近描述中的RN-AdS黑洞极为相似，例如允许等电荷的温度-熵相变，且缺乏等电压的温度-熵相变。在零电荷极限下，准局域描述中出现了渐近描述中不存在的Hawking-Page类相变。我们还简要讨论了RN-AdS黑洞的准局域行为，其与渐近描述定性相同，仅存在定量差异。

English: We extend the restricted phase space formalism for spherically symmetric black hole solutions of Einstein-Maxwell theory to the quasi-local regime, with the static observers located at a finite radial distance. The first law and Euler relation for the RN and RN-AdS black holes are proved to hold, but only with the inclusion of an extra pair of thermodynamic variables, {\em i.e.} the pressure $P$ and the area $A$ of the codimension-2 hypersurface on which the observers reside. For the RN black holes, the quasi-local behavior is analyzed in detail. It turns out that the RN black holes in the quasi-local description behaves significantly different from itself in the asymptotic description, but is extremely similar to the RN-AdS black holes in the asymptotic description, {\em e.g.} allowing for isocharge temperature-entropy phase transitions and lack of isovoltage temperature-entropy phase transitions. In the neutral limit, the Hawking-Page-like transitions appear in the quasi-local description which is absent in the asymptotic description. The quasi-local behavior of RN-AdS black holes is also briefly discussed, which is qualitatively identical to the asymptotic description with only quantitative differences.

一句话导读：将限制相空间形式推广到准局域有限距离观测者，发现RN黑洞在准局域下呈现类似RN-AdS的相变行为。

相关度：medium — 论文涉及黑洞热力学与相变，但未直接涉及全息或用户重点标签中的具体方向（如超流、涡旋等），仅与“相变与气泡成核”和“经典引力/广义相对论”相关。

德西特时空中高维圈量子黑洞：准正则模式与阴影特征

Higher Dimensional Loop Quantum Black hole in de Sitter Spacetime: Quasinormal Modes and Shadow Signatures

Sara Saghafi, Kourosh Nozari, Ali Mohammadpour · gr-qc, hep-ph, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：我们研究了德西特背景下高维圈量子修正黑洞的动力学和光学性质。首先分析视界结构，确定由极端和Nariai构型所界的允许非极端黑洞域，确保在所考虑参数集下存在不同的内视界、事件视界和宇宙学视界。接着考察标量有效势，表明圈量子修正主要在强场区域使经典散射势垒变形，同时保持其典型单势垒结构。使用时间域演化配合Prony提取、矩阵方法和WKB近似计算无质量标量扰动的准正则模式，三种方法吻合良好。时间域波形及其Prony和矩阵频率重构为提取的振铃谱提供额外直接一致性检验。我们发现圈量子修正对准正则谱产生中等偏移，而时空维度影响更强，导致更高的振荡频率和阻尼率。所有模式的负虚部表明在所探索参数范围内对标量扰动动力学稳定。与相应经典黑洞背景比较显示量子修正准正则谱与经典光子球分支连续连接，圈量子修正产生定量而非定性变化。

English: We investigate the dynamical and optical properties of a higher-dimensional loop-quantum-corrected black hole in a de Sitter background. We first analyze the horizon structure and identify the admissible nonextremal black-hole domain bounded by the extremal and Nariai configurations, ensuring the existence of distinct inner, event, and cosmological horizons for the parameter sets considered. We then examine the scalar effective potential and show that the loop-quantum correction deforms the classical scattering barrier primarily in the strong-field region while preserving its characteristic single-barrier structure. The quasinormal modes of massless scalar perturbations are computed using time-domain evolution with Prony extraction, the matrix method, and the WKB approximation, showing good agreement among the three approaches. The time-domain waveform and its Prony and matrix-frequency reconstructions provide an additional direct consistency check of the extracted ringdown spectrum. We find that loop quantum corrections induce moderate shifts in the quasinormal spectrum, whereas the spacetime dimensionality has a much stronger impact, leading to higher oscillation frequencies and damping rates. The negative imaginary parts of all modes indicate dynamical stability against massless scalar perturbations within the explored parameter range. Comparison with the corresponding classical black-hole backgrounds shows that the quantum-corrected quasinormal spectrum remains continuously connected to the classical photon-sphere branch, with the loop correction producing quantitative rather than qualitative changes.

一句话导读：研究德西特时空中高维圈量子黑洞的准正则模式，发现圈量子修正为定量修正，维度影响更显著且模式稳定。

相关度：high — 论文研究黑洞时空准正则模式，属于经典引力/广义相对论，并直接涉及用户关注的QNM支线。

推导动力学引力系统的有效描述与信号预测

Deriving effective descriptions and signal predictions for dynamical gravitational systems

Steven B. Giddings, Madhur Mehta · gr-qc, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：我们研究从顶层推导引力系统（如双星）辐射的有效描述。在指定截断方案下，可以推导世界线有效场论，尽管相关的截断依赖性可能使其表述复杂化。我们研究一种相关的有效方法，其中动力学由包围单个天体的空腔边界上的作用量参数化。我们为黑洞和修改黑洞行为的简单模型提供了此类空腔描述的示例。我们还展示了空腔有效描述如何与可观测物理量联系起来，包括Love数以及波形的各个方面，例如发射信号的累积相移。主要动机是开发一个系统框架，用于推断经典黑洞行为的修改如何影响引力波信号。这些修改可能源于黑洞演化必须与量子力学一致的要求，或其他新黑洞行为模型。特别是，旋进期间的累积相移被认为对小的新效应具有敏感性。为了清楚地说明基本原理，我们主要针对标量辐射发展该形式体系，然后概述其扩展到引力扰动。

English: We investigate top-down derivations of effective descriptions of radiation from gravitational systems such as binaries. With a specified cutoff prescription, one can derive worldline effective field theories, although the associated cutoff dependence can complicate their formulation. We investigate a related effective approach in which the dynamics are parameterized by an action on the boundaries of cavities enclosing the individual bodies. We present examples of such cavity descriptions for black holes and for simple models of modified black hole behavior. We also show how cavity effective descriptions connect to observable quantities including Love numbers and aspects of wave profiles, such as the accumulated phase shifts of emitted signals. A primary motivation is to develop a systematic framework for inferring how modifications of classical black hole behavior affect gravitational wave signals. Such modifications may be motivated by the requirement that black hole evolution be consistent with quantum mechanics, or by other models of new black hole behavior. Accumulated phase shifts during the inspiral have, in particular, been argued to provide sensitivity to small new effects. To illustrate the basic principles clearly, we develop the formalism primarily for scalar radiation, and then outline its extension to gravitational perturbations.

一句话导读：提出空腔有效描述方法，连接黑洞修改与引力波可观测信号。

相关度：medium — 论文聚焦经典引力与黑洞有效描述及引力波信号，属于经典引力研究支线，但与用户指定的全息相关支线无直接关联。

双组分量子暗费米子恒星的广义相对论结构

General-relativistic structure of two-component quantum dark fermion stars

Il\'{i}dio Lopes · astro-ph.HE, astro-ph.GA, gr-qc, hep-ph, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：我们发展了一个双组分量子暗费米子恒星的广义相对论框架：两种简并费米子物种的平衡构型，受引力、汤川相互作用介导的暗第五种力和全局相关的玻姆量子压修正支配。该处理保留了施瓦西内部非线性克莱因-戈登方程的完全协变形式，闭合关系在任意紧凑度下有效，从超轻基线到约0.16 fm^-3的密度（此时相对论标量密度和自洽有效费米子质量不可避免）。两个拉格朗日参数——暗费米子质量以及汤川道与引力的无量纲比——共同决定平衡结构；因此，质量-半径联合测量可直接从引力波可观测值约束暗区微观物理。对于10^-11至10^-10 eV的粒子质量，构型呈现3-24 km的半径和0.14-0.34的紧凑度，表现出双重模拟体行为：在中等等紧凑度时，它们在中子星的质量、半径和螺旋频率上重叠；在最紧凑端，它们穿过光子球，可能在质量间隙区域伪装成低质量黑洞。潮汐形变测量可区分这两类群体：无量纲潮汐形变明显小于中子星值但非零，不同于真正的黑洞。这些天体填充LISA、爱因斯坦望远镜和宇宙探索者的灵敏度带，并产生可由盖亚卫星单独分辨的天体测量微引力透镜信号。该框架为通过多信使观测约束暗区性质提供了可重复且可证伪的模板。

English: We develop a general-relativistic framework for two-component quantum dark fermion stars: equilibrium configurations of two degenerate fermion species governed by gravity, a Yukawa-mediated dark fifth force and a globally relevant Bohm quantum-pressure correction. The treatment retains the full covariant form of the nonlinear Klein--Gordon equation in the Schwarzschild interior, with closure relations valid at arbitrary compactness, from the ultralight baseline up to densities of order $0.16\,\mathrm{fm}^{-3}$ at which relativistic scalar densities and self-consistent effective fermion masses become unavoidable. Two Lagrangian parameters, the dark-fermion mass and the dimensionless ratio between the Yukawa channel and gravity, together fix the equilibrium structure; a joint measurement of mass and radius therefore constrains the dark-sector microphysics directly from gravitational-wave observables. For particle masses in the band $10^{-11}$--$10^{-10}$ eV, the configurations exhibit radii of $3$-$24\,\mathrm{km}$ and compactness in the range $0.14$-$0.34$, behaving as dual mimickers: at moderate compactness they overlap with neutron stars in mass, radius, and inspiral frequency; at the most compact end ($\kappa\sim 0.34$, $R_T/R_S\simeq 1.5$) they cross the photon sphere and could masquerade as low-mass black holes in the mass-gap region. Tidal-deformability measurements discriminate against both populations: the dimensionless tidal deformability is measurably smaller than the neutron-star value yet remains non-zero, unlike that of a genuine black hole. These objects populate the sensitivity bands of LISA, the Einstein Telescope, and Cosmic Explorer, producing astrometric microlensing signatures individually resolvable by \textit{Gaia}. The framework thus provides a reproducible and falsifiable template for constraining dark-sector properties through multi-messenger observations.

一句话导读：提出双组分量子暗费米子恒星的广义相对论平衡态模型，并讨论其作为中子星或低质量黑洞模拟体的可观测特征。

相关度：medium — 论文主要研究经典引力下致密星的结构与可观测特征，属于广义相对论应用，但并非全息或量子引力研究，与用户主要关注的全息支线无直接关联。

来自五维时空的引力与电磁学中的关联宇称破坏

Correlated parity violation in gravity and electromagnetism from five-dimensional spacetime

Haomin Rao, Yunlong Zheng, Qi-Zhe Hou, Jian-Wen Ou, Changyong Zhu · gr-qc, astro-ph.CO, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：引力与电磁领域的宇称破坏已被广泛研究，但两者通常被视为独立现象。然而，这种分离可能是四维偏见。在更高维时空中，引力与电磁可能共享几何起源——或许它们的宇称破坏也是如此。本文通过构造黎曼几何和teleparallel几何中的宇称破坏Kaluza-Klein模型来探讨这一想法。在黎曼几何中，最简单的五维宇称破坏项约化为多个复杂的四维项，包括已知的引力Chern-Simons项，该项存在鬼不稳定性。但在teleparallel几何中，结果异常简洁：最简单的五维宇称破坏项仅约化为两个无鬼项——已知的Nieh-Yan项和标准电磁Chern-Simons项。值得注意的是，该模型预言电磁波的螺旋度相关色散偏移正好是引力波的六倍：Δω²_EM = 6 Δω²_GW，这是一个与背景无关的关系。该关系为通过联合宇宙微波背景和引力波双折射观测检验统一理论提供了可证伪的途径。

English: Parity violation in the gravitational and electromagnetic sectors has been extensively investigated, yet the two are conventionally treated as independent phenomena. This separation, however, may be a four-dimensional prejudice. In higher-dimensional spacetime, gravity and electromagnetism may share a common geometric origin---and so, perhaps, does their parity violation. In this paper, we pursue this idea by constructing parity-violating Kaluza-Klein models in both Riemannian and teleparallel geometries. In Riemannian geometry, the simplest five-dimensional parity-violating term reduces to several complicated four-dimensional terms, including the familiar gravitational Chern-Simons term, which suffers from a ghost instability. In teleparallel geometry, however, the result is strikingly simple. The simplest five-dimensional parity-violating term reduces to only two ghost-free terms---the familiar Nieh-Yan term and the standard electromagnetic Chern-Simons term. Remarkably, the model predicts that the helicity-dependent dispersion shift for electromagnetic waves is exactly six times that for gravitational waves, $\Delta\omega^{2}_{ EM}=6\,\Delta\omega^{2}_{GW}$, a background-independent relation. This relation offers a falsifiable test of unification through joint cosmic microwave background and gravitational wave birefringence observations.

一句话导读：提出五维Kaluza-Klein模型，在teleparallel几何中宇称破坏约化为无鬼的Nieh-Yan项和电磁Chern-Simons项，并预言电磁波与引力波的双折射色散比值为6。

相关度：high — 论文主要研究经典引力与电磁学的高维统一模型，属于经典引力/广义相对论方向，与用户重点标签直接相关。

中子星外壳物态方程

Outer-crust equations of state for neutron stars

P. S. Koliogiannis, N. Paar · nucl-th, astro-ph.HE, astro-ph.SR · v3 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：中子星外壳的状态方程对核质量预言敏感，并提供了与整个核素图上原子核性质（包括实验难以达到的区域）的直接联系。我们量化了当代核质量模型对外壳组成和热力学性质的影响，并评估了在最小质量极限附近由外壳主导的中子星结构所受到的后果。我们基于相对论能量密度泛函和机器学习质量模型表构建了四个外壳状态方程。通过最小化每个核子的吉布斯自由能，得到了冷催化物质在β平衡下的平衡组成，并将得到的状态方程用于中子星结构计算。不同的质量输入导致平衡核素序列的变化、不同的最后束缚核以及中子滴线密度的适度移动。相比之下，相关的热力学性质以及最小质量中子星结构在四个外壳状态方程之间保持高度一致。外壳的模型依赖性主要体现在详细核素组成和中子滴线的精确位置上。尽管如此，所考虑的外壳状态方程对相关中子星可观测量给出高度一致的预言，为恒星建模提供了可靠输入。

English: The equation of state of the outer crust of neutron stars is sensitive to nuclear mass predictions and provides a direct connection to the properties of nuclei throughout the nuclide map, including those beyond experimental reach. We quantify the impact of contemporary nuclear mass models on the composition and thermodynamic properties of the outer crust and assess the consequences for crust-dominated neutron-star configurations near the minimum-mass limit. We constructed four outer-crust equations of state based on the relativistic energy density functional and machine-learning mass model tables. The equilibrium composition of cold catalysed matter in $\beta$-equilibrium was obtained by minimising the Gibbs free energy per nucleon, and the resulting equations of state were implemented in neutron-star structure calculations. The different mass inputs lead to variations in the equilibrium nuclide sequence, distinct last bound nuclei, and moderate shifts in the neutron-drip density. In contrast, the associated thermodynamic properties, as well as the minimum-mass neutron-star configurations, remain closely aligned across the four outer-crust equations of state. The model dependence of the outer crust is primarily reflected in the detailed nuclide composition and in the precise location of neutron drip. Nevertheless, the considered outer-crust equations of state yield closely consistent predictions for the relevant neutron-star observables, providing a reliable input for stellar modelling.

一句话导读：基于不同核质量模型构建中子星外壳状态方程，并评估其对最小质量中子星结构的影响。

相关度：high — 论文研究中子星结构中的状态方程与引力平衡，属于经典引力/广义相对论在天体物理中的应用，与用户重点标签中的经典引力支线直接相关。

时空奇点与无穷远区域之间的关系

The relationship between spacetime singularities and regions at infinity

Junbang Liu, Ben Andrews, Susan M Scott · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：理想附着点是广义相对论中伪黎曼流形的核心概念，时空能否在满足一定性质下延拓是选择附着点时的中心考量。本文对任何极大延拓的伪黎曼流形建立了奇点与无穷远点可分离的充分条件。我们关注 (M,g) 的不完全测地线，并构造时空的一个包络 (M,g,\hat{M})，使得不完全测地线 γ:[0,1)→M 在 \hat{M} 中有端点 q。如果没有一对趋近 q 的测地线相互交织，则 q 是奇点。此外，q 不会被任何具有无限仿射参数的测地线趋近，因此不能覆盖无穷远点，从而在抽象边界框架中成为“纯奇点”。我们将端点定理应用于 Graf 和 Beld-Serrano 在 arXiv:2307.11034 中引入的极大 g-边界，并给出方向奇点与纯奇点之间可分离性的结果。该分析随后被应用于 Schwarzschild 时空。

English: Ideal attached points are a core concept in general relativity for pseudo-Riemannian manifolds, and whether the spacetime can be extended with certain properties is a central consideration in their choice. This paper establishes a sufficient condition for the separability between singularities and points at infinity for any maximally extended pseudo-Riemannian manifold. We focus on the incomplete geodesics of $(\mathcal{M},g)$, and produce an envelopment $(\mathcal{M},g,\hat{\mathcal{M}})$ of the spacetime such that an incomplete geodesic $\gamma:[0,1) \rightarrow \mathcal{M}$ has an endpoint $q$ in $\hat{\mathcal{M}}$. If there is no pair of geodesics approaching $q$ which is intertwined, then $q$ is a singularity. Additionally, $q$ will not be approached by any geodesic with infinite affine parameter, and therefore cannot cover a point at infinity, thereby rendering it a {\it pure singularity} in the abstract boundary framework. We apply the Endpoint Theorem to the maximal g-boundary introduced by Graf and Beld-Serrano in arXiv:2307.11034, and also provide a result on the separability between directional singularities and pure singularities. This analysis is then applied to the Schwarzschild spacetime.

一句话导读：该论文在经典广义相对论的抽象边界框架下，证明了一个关于时空奇点与无穷远区域可分离性的充分条件，并将其应用于 Schwarzschild 时空。

相关度：low — 该论文是纯经典广义相对论关于时空奇点与无穷远边界的研究，完全不涉及全息对偶、量子引力或量子信息，与用户关注的全息及相关方向无重叠。

在Minkowski超双曲叶状结构上的三维求和-分部方案

A 3D Summation-by-Parts scheme on a Hyperboloidal Foliation of Minkowski

Shalabh Gautam · gr-qc, cs.NA, math-ph, math.AP, math.MP, math.NA · v1 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：本文总结了我们先前的工作：针对固定Minkowski背景上超双曲切片的线性波动方程，推导了一种全三维求和-分部（SBP）格式。该格式在球极坐标中推导，允许在原点、z轴（尽管有坐标奇异性）以及无穷远处设置网格点，通过引入紧致化并重新标度来实现，并证明其稳定性。将其简化为标准柯西问题或具有外边界的有限类空切片的方法类似。实现该格式时采用二阶精度有限差分方法，但也可以使用高阶有限差分或谱方法。Kreiss-Oliger耗散算子被推广到曲线坐标，并定义在域内各处，包括边界点，使其在能量范数下满足耗散性质。我们还提出了新的范数收敛测试，在所有分辨率下包含所有网格点，给出更精确的结果。获得了有希望的结果，为应用于完全非线性的系统（如爱因斯坦场方程）并从系统误差或规范歧义中提取引力波带来了希望。

English: This paper summarises our previous work on a fully $3$D Summation-by-Parts scheme, derived for a class of linear wave equations on hyperboloidal slices on a fixed Minkowski background. The scheme is derived in spherical polar coordinates, and allows having grid points at the origin and on the $z$-axis, despite coordinate singularities, and at infinity, by introducing compactification followed by rescaling, and is proved to be stable. Reducing it to the standard Cauchy problem, or to finite spacelike slices with an outer boundary, will follow a similarly. Second-order accurate finite-difference methods are used to implement this scheme numerically, but higher-order finite-difference or spectral methods could also be used. Kreiss-Oliger dissipation operators are generalized to curvilinear coordinates and are defined everywhere in the domain, including at the boundary points, such that they satisfy the dissipative property in the energy norms. We also propose new norm convergence tests that include all the grid points at all resolutions and produce more accurate results. Promising results are obtained, giving hope for application to fully nonlinear systems, like the Einstein Field Equations, and extracting the resulting gravitational waves free of systematic errors or gauge ambiguities.

一句话导读：提出一种在Minkowski超双曲切片上的三维SBP数值格式，稳定且可推广到非线性爱因斯坦方程。

一类广义Taub-NUT-标量度规

A Generalized Class of Taub-NUT-Scalar Metrics

Fataneh Bakherad, Behrouz Mirza, Reza Bahani, Mahnaz Tavakoli Kachi, Ali Derekeh · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：我们考虑了一类三参数度规，该度规在\cite{azizallahi2024three}中引入。这类度规在特定参数值下包含Zipoy-Voorhees (ZV)和Fisher-Janis-Newman-Winicour (FJNW)时空。我们通过Ehlers变换将Newman-Unti-Tamburino (NUT)电荷引入这类度规，构建了一个具有引力磁效应的轴对称渐近平坦时空。我们研究了这类Taub-NUT-标量(TNS)度规的物理特性，包括引力透镜、准正则模(QNM)谱和测试粒子运动。我们的结果为质量、多极矩和引力磁场在标量场存在时的行为提供了新的见解，并展示了NUT电荷对这类度规中轨道稳定性的影响。

English: We consider a class of three-parameter metrics that was introduced in \cite{azizallahi2024three}. The class of metrics contain Zipoy-Voorhees (ZV) and Fisher-Janis-Newman- Winicour (FJNW) spacetimes at certain values of the parameters. We investigate the physical features of this class of Taub-NUT-scalar (TNS) metrics including gravitational lensing, quasinormal mode (QNM) spectra, and test particle motion. 弱引力与规范背景中的粒子对产生：自旋场与双星

Pair creation in weak gravity and gauge backgrounds: spinning fields and binary stars

中文摘要：我们研究自旋 0、1/2 和 1 的量子场在弱引力与规范背景中的粒子对产生。利用重求和热核技术，我们得到了对产生概率在“广义曲率”二次阶内的闭合表达式。此外，对于引力背景，我们将对产生概率重新表述为与爱因斯坦方程自洽所需的物质分布。最后，为说明方法的有效性，我们分析了双星系统中粒子对产生的效应。

English: We study pair creation for quantum fields of spin $0$, $1/2$, and $1$ in the presence of weak gravitational and gauge backgrounds. Using resummed heat-kernel techniques, we obtain closed expressions for the pair creation probability up to quadratic order in the ``generalized curvatures''. In addition, for gravitational backgrounds, we recast the pair production probability in terms of the matter distribution required for consistency with the Einstein equation. Finally, to illustrate the powerfulness of our methods, we analyze the effect of pair creation in a binary star system.

扭秤作为半经典引力的操作探针：匹配滤波界限、扭矩扩散约束和量子噪声基准

Torsion balances as operational probes of semiclassical gravity: Matched-filter bounds, torque-diffusion constraints, and quantum-noise benchmarks

Jyotirmaya Mohanta, Yutaka Shikano · gr-qc, quant-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：校准扭秤谱约束相对于标准牛顿引力的确定性和随机偏差。利用单边谱，我们推导了一个校准的等效角度噪声预算，以及已知扭矩模板的有限时间匹配滤波/Cramér-Rao界限。对于随机模型，从观测的角度谱中减去校准的标准噪声预算（热噪声、牛顿噪声、环境噪声、不精确性、反作用力），得到残差谱，通过校准的扭转响应可转换为等效的残差扭矩谱。这限制了额外的平稳随机扭矩噪声。这种频分辨约束仅在马尔可夫白噪声极限下压缩为单一的扭矩扩散系数 D_tau；非马尔可夫或彩色模型需要完整的残差谱。Page–Geilker 分支判别和 Fedida–Kent 混合等价检验针对不同的物理问题，但在投影到扭矩模板后，两者都归结为相同的统计匹配滤波判别问题。对于室温 Cavendish 基准，共振热角度 ASD 为 1.36e-4 rad/√Hz，而测量附加 SQL 为 3.25e-12 rad/√Hz。对于 Yan 等人的搜索，在 2.5 mHz 处报告的 0.3 μrad/√Hz 灵敏度，在假设白扭矩噪声的情况下，给出了保守界限 D_tau ≲ 2.4e-23 N²m²s。这些表述提供了一个接口，将校准扭秤数据、确定性测试和随机半经典引力搜索联系起来，而无需断言对完整相对论性半经典 Einstein 方程的直接检验。

English: Calibrated torsion-balance spectra constrain deterministic and stochastic deviations from standard Newtonian gravity. Using one-sided spectra, we derive a calibrated angle-equivalent noise budget and finite-time matched-filter/Cram\'er-Rao bounds for known torque templates. For stochastic models, subtracting the calibrated standard noise budget (thermal, Newtonian, environmental, imprecision, backaction) from the observed angle spectrum yields a residual spectrum, convertible to an equivalent residual torque spectrum via calibrated torsional susceptibility. This bounds additional stationary stochastic torque noise. This frequency-resolved bound compresses to a single torque-diffusion coefficient, $D_\tau$, only in the Markovian white-noise limit; non-Markovian or colored models require the full residual spectrum. Page--Geilker branch discrimination and Fedida-Kent mixture-equivalence tests address distinct physical questions, but upon projection onto torque templates, both reduce to the same statistical matched-filter discrimination problem. For a room-temperature Cavendish benchmark, resonant thermal angle ASD is $1.36\times10^{-4}\,\mathrm{rad}/\sqrt{\mathrm{Hz}}$, while measurement-added SQL is $3.25\times10^{-12}\,\mathrm{rad}/\sqrt{\mathrm{Hz}}$. For the Yan \emph{et al.} search, the reported $0.3\,\mu\mathrm{rad}/\sqrt{\mathrm{Hz}}$ sensitivity at $2.5\,\mathrm{mHz}$ yields a conservative bound $D_\tau \lesssim 2.4\times10^{-23}\,\mathrm{N^2\,m^2\,s}$, assuming white torque noise. These formulations provide an interface linking calibrated torsion-balance data, deterministic tests, and stochastic semiclassical-gravity searches, without asserting a direct test of the full relativistic semiclassical Einstein equation.

一句话导读：利用校准扭秤谱约束半经典引力的确定性/随机偏差，并推导扭矩噪声的匹配滤波、扩散系数和量子噪声界限。

相关度：medium — 论文涉及扭秤实验对经典牛顿引力偏差和半经典引力效应的测试，与用户重点中的经典引力/广义相对论支线有一定关联；但不涉及全息、凝聚态等核心研究线。

首批连续引力波的性质：优化脉冲星计时观测以探测超大质量黑洞双星

The properties of the first continuous gravitational waves: Optimizing pulsar timing observations for supermassive black hole binary detection

Blaze Houlden, Eric Thrane, Katie Auchettl · astro-ph.HE, astro-ph.CO, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：多个脉冲星计时阵列已报告了纳赫兹引力波背景的证据。该背景被认为来源于一群旋近的超大质量黑洞双星信号的叠加。检验这一解释的关键是寻找其信号突出于该背景之上的可单独分辨的双星。这里我们通过解决两个相关问题来研究这种可能性：(1) 第一个可分辨双星最可能具有什么性质；(2) 如何优化脉冲星计时观测，以最大化其电磁证认的前景。通过模拟受引力波背景证据约束的超大质量黑洞双星种群，并研究每次模拟中最响亮的双星，我们推断出首批可单独分辨系统的预期性质。如果MeerKAT继续当前观测策略总计9年，探测到单个双星的概率约为10-27%。将计时精度提高一倍，该概率变为约11-28%；而将观测频率提高四倍，则给出约12-30%。两者结合改进得到约12-33%的探测概率。此外，实施高频观测活动优先解析较高频率（≳10 nHz）的系统，其较短的轨道周期使得它们比低频重复较少的天体更容易通过电磁观测来识别。我们讨论了利用当前高频全天光学巡天对引力波分辨双星进行电磁后续观测的前景。

English: Several pulsar timing arrays have reported evidence for a nanohertz gravitational-wave background. This background is thought to arise from the superposition of signals from a population of inspiraling supermassive black hole binaries. A key test of this interpretation is the search for individually resolvable binaries whose signals emerge above this background. Here we investigate this possibility by addressing two related questions: (1) what are the most probable properties of the first resolvable binary; and (2) how can pulsar timing observations be optimized to maximize its prospects for electromagnetic identification? By simulating populations of supermassive black hole binaries constrained by evidence for the gravitational-wave background and studying the loudest binary in each simulation, we infer the expected properties of the first individually resolvable systems. If MeerKAT continues its current observing strategy for a total of 9 years, the probability of detecting an individual binary is $\approx10-27\%$. Improving the timing precision by a factor of two changes this probability to $\approx11-28\%$, whereas increasing the cadence by a factor of four gives $\approx12-30\%$. Combining both improvements yields a detection probability of $\approx12-33\%$. Furthermore, implementing a high-cadence observing campaign preferentially resolves higher-frequency systems ($\gtrsim\unit[10]{nHz}$), whose shorter orbital periods make them considerably easier to identify through electromagnetic observations than lower-frequency binaries that repeat infrequently. We discuss prospects of electromagnetic follow-up of gravitational-wave resolved binaries with current high-cadence all-sky optical surveys.

一句话导读：通过模拟推断首个可分辨超大质量黑洞双星的性质，并优化脉冲星计时观测以提高探测与电磁证认概率。

相关度：low — 该论文研究引力波天文学中的脉冲星计时和超大质量黑洞双星探测，与理论全息或经典引力理论的核心问题关联较弱。

Kerr时空中的双模与Whiting变换：模式稳定性再探

Dual modes in Kerr spacetimes and the Whiting transform: Mode stability revisited

Oliver Petersen, Andr\'as Vasy · math.AP, gr-qc, math.DG · v1 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：本文旨在将Whiting经典的增模稳定性论证（Shlapentokh-Rothman将其推广到标量波动方程的实频率情形，Andersson、Ma、Paganini和Whiting将其推广到一般情形）置于经典PDE理论框架之中。关键步骤是：对偶模或伴随模的描述、通过傅里叶变换实现的奇异相空间配对论证，以及随后标准唯一延拓结果。我们对对偶模的描述之一将其直接与在事件视界上光滑的标准模式解联系起来。在零自旋情形下，这有一个几何解释：横跨未来事件视界光滑的（非零实频率）拟正规模可以通过0延拓穿过过去事件视界，成为分布意义的QNM解。我们还描述了模式解在分叉球处的行为。

English: The purpose of the paper is to place Whiting's classical growing mode stability argument, extended to real frequencies by Shlapentokh-Rothman for the scalar wave equation and by Andersson, Ma, Paganini and Whiting in general, in the framework of classical PDE theory. The key steps are: a description of the dual or adjoint modes, a singular phase space pairing argument which is technically executed via the Fourier transform, followed by a standard unique continuation result. One part of our description of the dual modes connects them directly to the standard mode solutions which are smooth over the event horizon. In the zero spin case, there is a geometric interpretation of this: quasinormal modes (for non-zero real frequencies) which are smooth across the future event horizon can be extended as distributional QNM solutions by 0 through the past event horizon. We also describe the behavior of mode solutions at the bifurcate sphere.

相关度：high — 论文研究Kerr时空中的经典引力模式稳定性与拟正规模，直接属于经典引力/广义相对论领域，并涉及QNM相关课题。

引力波对原子氢射电谱线的影响：超精细分裂与展宽机制

Gravitational Wave Effects on Radio Spectral Lines of Atomic Hydrogen: Hyperfine Splitting and Broadening Mechanisms

Nontapat Wanwieng, Nithiwadee Thaicharoen, Narupon Chattrapiban, Apimook Watcharangkool · gr-qc, astro-ph.HE, quant-ph · v2 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：我们探讨了引力波对氢射电谱线的影响，重点关注基态超精细跃迁和高度激发的里德伯态中的辐射跃迁。为了分析引力波对超精细结构的影响，我们使用线性化引力和3+1形式推导了引力波背景下的麦克斯韦方程组。我们的发现表明，引力波会诱导超精细磁子能级的能量移动，从而修改21厘米谱线。然而，这些能量移动远低于当前射电天文仪器的探测极限。对于高度激发态的跃迁（产生射电复合线，RRL），引力波的影响表现为谱线展宽，其中Hnα谱线的分数线宽近似为Δν/ν0 ~ n^7 ω_gw^2 h(t)。这表明射电复合线可以作为超高频率引力波的探针，尤其是考虑到星际介质中的里德伯原子量子数可超过n=100。作为一个可能可探测的高频引力波源示例，我们研究了行星质量原初黑洞双星并合过程中发射的引力波，其中引力波引起的射电复合线展宽可能超过自然展宽效应。此外，我们还研究了最近探测到的随机引力波背景对氢谱线的影响。

English: We explore the effects of gravitational waves (GWs) on hydrogen's radio spectral lines, focusing on the ground-state hyperfine transition and radiative transitions in highly excited Rydberg states. To analyze GW impacts on hyperfine structure, we derive Maxwell's equations in a gravitational-wave background using linearized gravity and the $3+1$ formalism. Our findings reveal that GWs induce energy shifts in hyperfine magnetic substates, modifying the 21 cm line. However, these energy shifts fall well below the detection limits of current radio astronomical instruments. For transitions in highly excited states, which produce radio recombination lines (RRL), the influence of GW manifests itself as spectral broadening, with the fractional linewidth for $\mathrm{H}n\alpha$ scaling as $\Delta\nu/\nu_0 \sim n^7\omega^2_{\mathrm{gw}}h(t)$. This suggests that RRLs could serve as probes for ultra-high-frequency GWs, particularly given that Rydberg atoms in the interstellar medium can reach quantum numbers above $n=100$. As an example of possibly detectable high frequency GW source, We investigate GWs emitted during the inspiral of planetary-mass primordial black hole binaries, where GW-induced broadening in RRLs could exceed natural broadening effects. Additionally, we examine the influence of the recently detected stochastic gravitational-wave background on hydrogen spectral lines.

一句话导读：研究引力波对氢21cm线和射电复合线的谱线展宽效应，提出里德伯原子可作为高频引力波探针。

相关度：low — 该论文研究经典引力波对原子谱线的影响，不涉及全息对偶或AdS/CFT，仅与经典引力/广义相对论支线相关，与用户主要全息研究方向不匹配。

偏心率的形状：利用小波散射变换快速分类偏心双星

The Shape of Eccentricity: Rapid Classification of Eccentric Binaries with the Wavelet Scattering Transform

Priscilla Canizares, Seppe J. Staelens, Isobel Romero-Shaw · gr-qc, astro-ph.HE · v2 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA（LVK）合作迄今报告的引力波（GW）探测与近圆双致密星并合（CBCs）一致。然而，在致密恒星环境或场三星通过动力学相互作用被驱动并合的少量双星，可能在进入LVK探测器灵敏频带时保留可测量的轨道偏心率。在LVK频带中可靠测量偏心率将为这类动力学驱动的并合提供有力证据；然而，偏心引力波形模型计算成本高昂，对全部探测信号进行生产级推断并非资源高效利用，因为偏心信号预期罕见。在探测与分析之间的中间步骤中，评估信号是否存在偏心，可为哪些信号应进行完整偏心推断提供快速建议。我们将小波散射变换（WST）应用于大量含实际噪声的合成波形，并使用简单线性和浅层神经网络分类器评估其判别能力。我们发现WST表示能有效区分偏心与近圆双星，并提供引力波信号的紧凑多尺度表示。我们的方法在10%误报率下达到约64%的探测准确率，AUC为0.844，平均精度为0.876。我们还检验了分类器区分偏心与自旋进动效应的能力，并在多种自旋进动幅度下发现稳健性能。

English: The gravitational-wave (GW) detections reported by the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA (LVK) collaboration have so far been consistent with quasi-circular compact binary coalescences (CBCs). Nevertheless, a small fraction of binaries driven to merge through dynamical interactions in dense stellar environments or in field triples may retain measurable orbital eccentricity when entering the sensitive frequency band of LVK detectors. Confident measurement of eccentricity in the LVK band would provide strong evidence for such dynamically driven mergers; however, eccentric gravitational-waveform models are computationally expensive, and performing production-level inference on all detected signals is not an efficient use of resources when eccentric signals are expected to be rare. An intermediate step between detection and analysis, in which the signal is assessed for the potential presence of eccentricity, could provide quick recommendations for which signals should undergo full eccentric inference. We apply the wavelet scattering transform (WST) to a large set of synthetic waveforms in realistic noise and assess its discriminatory power using simple linear and shallow neural-network classifiers. We find that the WST representation enables effective discrimination between eccentric and quasi-circular binaries and provides a compact multi-scale representation of GW signals. Our approach achieves ~64% percent detection accuracy at a false alarm rate of 10%, with an AUC of 0.844 and an average precision of 0.876. We also examine the ability of our classifiers to distinguish eccentricity from spin-induced precession and find robust performance across a range of spin-precession magnitudes.

一句话导读：利用小波散射变换对引力波信号进行快速分类，以识别偏心双星候选体，节省计算资源。

相关度：medium — 论文涉及经典引力波数据处理与分类，属于引力波天文学方法研究，与全息或量子引力无关。

含高阶谐波与偏心率的致密双星视线加速度

Line-of-sight acceleration in compact binaries with higher harmonics and eccentricity

Soumen Roy, Justin Janquart · gr-qc, astro-ph.HE · v2 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：引力波的直接探测为探测致密双星并合的天体物理起源提供了独特机会。这类系统的形成渠道仍存在很大争议，部分可能源于动力学环境或活动星系核。在此类环境中形成的双星预计会受到周围环境引起的视线加速度影响，这可能在观测到的引力波信号上留下特征印记。本文重新推导了视线加速度效应，并将其实现到具有进动和高阶模的准圆波形模型中。我们还在偏心率波形模型中实现了这些修正，并一致地应用于所有贡献谐波。利用该模型，我们研究了这些效应对视线加速度推断的影响，并分析了LIGO和Virgo第三次观测运行期间观测到的若干有趣的GWTC事件。我们在这些事件中未发现视线加速度的实质性证据。我们还表明，高阶谐波之间对视线加速度的不一致处理可能导致有偏的结论。我们的模型为在当前和未来引力波观测中发现视线加速度提供了一个稳健框架，从而能够更准确地探测致密双星形成中的环境特征。

English: Direct detections of gravitational waves provide a unique opportunity to probe the astrophysical origin of compact binary mergers. The formation channels of these systems remain highly debated, and a fraction may originate in dynamical environments or active galactic nuclei. Binaries formed in such environments are expected to experience line-of-sight acceleration from their surroundings, which can imprint characteristic signatures on the observed gravitational-wave signal. Here, we re-derive the line-of-sight acceleration effects and implement them in state-of-the-art quasi-circular waveform models with precession and higher-order modes. We also implement the corrections in eccentric waveform models, applying them consistently to all contributing harmonics. Using this model, we investigate the impact of these effects on the inference of line-of-sight acceleration and analyze a few interesting GWTC events observed during the third observing run of LIGO and Virgo. We find no substantial evidence for line-of-sight acceleration in these events. We also show that an inconsistent treatment of line-of-sight acceleration between higher harmonics can lead to biased conclusions. Our model provides a robust framework for uncovering line-of-sight acceleration in current and future gravitational-wave observations, enabling more accurate probes of environmental signatures in compact-binary formation.

一句话导读：将视线加速度效应一致地纳入含高阶谐波和偏心率的致密双星引力波波形模型，并分析LIGO/Virgo GWTC事件未发现显著证据。

相关度：high — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论中的引力波波形建模与致密双星环境效应研究，与用户标记的经典引力支线直接相关；但非全息或量子引力。

全局双曲性与阶完备性

Global hyperbolicity meets order completeness

E. Minguzzi · gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：最近，在一些低正则时空几何文献中，一个阶完备性性质引起了关注，被称为“时序完备性”。在这项工作中，我们证明在标准洛伦兹几何框架下，该性质等价于全局双曲性。同时建立了全局双曲性与其他更传统的阶完备性形式的等价性。

English: Recently, an order completeness property has attracted attention in some low regularity spacetime geometry literature, where it was named `chronocompleteness'. In this work we prove that, in the framework of standard Lorentzian geometry, this property is equivalent to global hyperbolicity. The equivalence of global hyperbolicity with other more traditional forms of order completeness is also established.

一句话导读：证明了在标准洛伦兹几何中，'chronocompleteness'（阶完备性）等价于全局双曲性。

相关度：medium — 论文研究洛伦兹几何中的全局双曲性与阶完备性的等价关系，属于经典引力/广义相对论中的数学基础问题，与全息/量子引力无关，但直接对应经典引力支线。

白矮星 ADM 质量对二次 f(Q) 引力的贝叶斯约束

Bayesian constraints on quadratic $f(Q)$ gravity from the ADM mass of white dwarfs

Edson Otoniel, Lucas A. Souza, Jonathan A. Rebou\c{c}as, Juan M. Z. Pretel · gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：我们在二次对称平行引力 f(Q)=Q+αQ² 中计算白矮星的静态平衡序列，并利用质量-半径测量对负耦合区、广义相对论和正耦合区进行贝叶斯比较。通过施加仿射场方程并选取解析分支 C=r exp(B/2) 得到正则内部解，并与渐近平坦外部时空匹配以确定 ADM 质量。物质部分由冷碳-12 状态方程描述，包含相对论电子简并、核静止能量和库仑晶格修正。中心密度在固定 α 下用对数均匀先验边缘化。序列覆盖 10^14 ≤ |α|/cm² ≤ 10^21。正耦合使致密分支向更大半径和更低质量移动，而负耦合产生高质量延展，包括一个 M_ADM=14.34 太阳质量的允许平衡结构。利用 Sirius B、QS Vir、V471 Tau B、ZTF J1901+1458 和 LHS 4033，我们得到条件后验中位数 α_- = -1.12×10^16 cm² 和 α_+ = 1.85×10^17 cm²。在等先验权重下，负、GR 和正耦合区的后验概率分别为 0.228、0.378 和 0.395，ln(B_+/GR)=0.043。因此，当前数据对耦合参数给出了条件约束，但没有对修改引力提供直接证据，强调在用白矮星观测约束该理论时必须一致地定义 ADM 质量方案和可允许恒星域。

English: We calculate static equilibrium sequences for white dwarfs (WDs) in quadratic symmetric teleparallel gravity, f(Q)=Q+alpha Q^2, and perform a Bayesian comparison between the negative-coupling sector, general relativity (GR), and the positive-coupling sector using mass-radius measurements. Regular interior solutions are obtained by imposing the affine field equation and selecting the analytic branch C=r exp(B/2), and are matched to asymptotically flat exterior spacetimes to determine the ADM mass. The matter sector is described by a cold carbon-12 equation of state including relativistic electron degeneracy, nuclear rest energy, and Coulomb lattice corrections. Central density is marginalized at fixed alpha with a log-uniform prior. The sequences span 10^14 <= |alpha|/cm^2 <= 10^21. Positive couplings shift the compact branch toward larger radii and lower masses, whereas negative couplings produce high-mass extensions, including an admissible equilibrium configuration with M_ADM=14.34 solar masses. Using Sirius B, QS Vir, V471 Tau B, ZTF J1901+1458, and LHS 4033, we obtain conditional posterior medians alpha_-= -1.12x10^16 cm^2 and alpha_+=1.85x10^17 cm^2. With equal prior weights, the posterior probabilities for the negative, GR, and positive sectors are 0.228, 0.378, and 0.395, respectively, with ln(B_+/GR)=0.043. Current data therefore provide conditional constraints on the coupling but no direct evidence for modified gravity, emphasizing that the ADM mass prescription and admissible stellar domain must be consistently defined when using WD observations to constrain the theory.

一句话导读：利用白矮星质量-半径测量对二次 f(Q) 引力进行贝叶斯约束，未发现修改引力的直接证据。

相关度：high — 论文研究二次 f(Q) 修改引力中的白矮星，属于经典引力/广义相对论范畴，与用户重点标签“经典引力/广义相对论”直接相关。

通过连续偏序集的时空：旧基础与新进展

Spacetimes via Continuous posets: Old foundations and new developments

Ettore Minguzzi · gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：我们向读者介绍连续偏序集方法处理时空几何，这一主题由 Martin 和 Panangaden 开创。我们提供来自标准域论来源的所有相关证明，以方便来自洛伦兹几何和广义相对论的读者过渡。然后，我们展示时空解释的新结果。在把 way-below 关系等同于时间序关系 I 的识别下，我们确定 ≤ 和因果条件的唯一选择以获得特定偏序集性质。连续偏序集需要 ≤=D_p 并对应于“过去区分性和未来反射性”；双连续偏序集是因果连续时空，≤=D；全局双曲偏序集正是全局双曲时空，≤=J，证明了超越 Martin 和 Panangaden 充分性的必要性结果。该理论完全以偏序集表述，具有低正则性。我们引入一种拓扑的 Kronheimer-Penrose 因果空间概念，它足够一般以包含文献中的洛伦兹长度空间，并给出使其成为（双）连续偏序集的弱条件。一旦时空成为连续偏序集，域论构造即可直接应用，例如完备化方案产生时空边界。我们回顾几个；最自然的保持连续性的 Lawson 的 round-ideal 完备化被证明等于 future GKP 完备化。Gigli 等人最近研究的 directed completion 也被证明等价于 future GKP 完备化；然而我们能够移除额外的 SC 条件和前向逼近。最后，关于过去反射性在黑洞蒸发中近期确立的关键作用的物理考虑使我们建议，时空在基本层面上是一个 co-continuous 偏序集。

English: We introduce the reader to the continuous posets approach to spacetime geometry, a topic pioneered by Martin and Panangaden. We provide all relevant proofs from standard domain-theory sources to ease the transition for readers from Lorentzian geometry and general relativity. We then present new results for the spacetime interpretation. Under the identification of the way-below relation with the chronological relation $I$, we determine the unique choice for $\le$ and causality condition to obtain specific poset properties. Continuous posets require $\le=D_p$ and correspond to "past-distinction and future-reflectivity"; bicontinuous posets are causally continuous spacetimes with $\le=D$; globally hyperbolic posets are precisely globally hyperbolic spacetimes with $\le=J$ proving a necessity result beyond Martin and Panangaden's sufficiency. The theory, being phrased entirely in terms of a poset, is of low-regularity. We introduce a notion of topological Kronheimer-Penrose causal space that is sufficiently general to encompass the Lorentzian length spaces in the literature, and give weak conditions making it a (bi)continuous poset. Once a spacetime is a continuous poset, domain-theoretic constructions apply directly, e.g., completion schemes yield spacetime boundaries. We recall a few; the most natural for preserving continuity, Lawson's round-ideal completion, is proved to equal the future GKP completion. The directed completion recently studied by Gigli et al. was also proved to be equivalent to the future GKP completion; however with additional SC condition and forward approximation which we are able to remove. Finally, physical considerations on the recently established key role of past-reflectivity in black hole evaporation lead us to suggest that the spacetime is, at the fundamental level, a co-continuous poset.

一句话导读：本文综述并发展了连续偏序集描述时空几何的方法，给出其与因果结构的严格对应，并指出其可能的物理应用。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论的数学基础研究，与用户经典引力支线相关，但不涉及具体全息或凝聚态模型。

天体物理引力波背景的能量上限与基准

Energetic Ceilings and Benchmarks of Astrophysical Gravitational-Wave Backgrounds

Chiara M. F. Mingarelli · astro-ph.HE, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：每个天体物理引力波背景（GWB）都受到其源能转化为引力辐射的静止质量的限制。我们结合了六类源的可用源质量、并合因子、辐射效率和并合时代：超大质量黑洞双星（SMBHBs）、极端质量比旋进（EMRIs）、中等质量黑洞旋进到超大质量黑洞、恒星质量双黑洞（sBBHs）、双中子星（BNSs）和Pop III双黑洞。对每一类，我们计算了一个GWB基准和一个上限。对于SMBHBs，Liepold & Ma (2024)的局部质量普查，结合Sato-Polito et al. (2024)的重复并合增强，使我们能够计算纳赫兹GWB的上限。将GWB锚定到该普查，并假设每个SMBH一次并合，给出基准为$A_{\rm bench} = 2.11^{+0.43}_{-0.32}\times10^{-15}$在$f=1\,{\rm yr}^{-1}$处。对于上限，我们假设无限次先前并合，得到$A_{\rm ceil}=3.76^{+0.76}_{-0.56}\times10^{-15}$，与已发表的PTA振幅一致。因此，在这种质量函数和并合模型下，测得的GWB振幅并不需要额外的SMBH质量。一个测得的GWB反而可以约束其源群体。使用修订后的NANOGrav 15年GWB振幅和该SMBH普查，给出$f_{\rm merge}=1.11^{+1.35}_{-0.63}$，其中1表示每个现今残余对应一次并合。对于其他五类源，尚不存在可比较的普查，因此我们根据最佳现有源约束报告上限，包括致密双星并合率。最后，六个采用的上限情形给出了现今引力波能量预算$\rho_{\rm gw}^{(6)}=3.33^{+1.32}_{-0.83}\times10^{-7}\rho_c$。更大的能量密度将需要更多源质量、并合、辐射效率或更晚的时代，或指向额外的源群体或新物理。

English: Every astrophysical gravitational-wave background (GWB) is limited by the rest mass its sources can convert into gravitational radiation. We combine available source mass, merger factor, radiative efficiency, and merger epochs for six populations: supermassive black hole binaries (SMBHBs), extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs), IMBHs inspiralling into SMBHs, stellar mass binary black holes (sBBHs), binary neutron stars (BNSs), and Pop~III binary black holes. For each, we calculate a GWB benchmark and an upper limit. For SMBHBs, the local mass census of Liepold \& Ma (2024), together with the repeated-merger boost of Sato-Polito et al. (2024), allows us to compute a ceiling on the nanoHertz GWB. Anchoring the GWB to this census, and assuming one merger per SMBH, gives a benchmark of $A_{\rm bench} = 2.11^{+0.43}_{-0.32}\times10^{-15}$ at $f=1\,{\rm yr}^{-1}$. For the ceiling, we assume an infinite number of previous mergers, yielding $A_{\rm ceil}=3.76^{+0.76}_{-0.56}\times10^{-15}$, consistent with published PTA amplitudes. Under this mass function and merger model, the measured GWB amplitude therefore does not require additional SMBH mass. A measured GWB can instead constrain its source population. Using the revised NANOGrav 15-yr GWB amplitude and this SMBH census gives $f_{\rm merge}=1.11^{+1.35}_{-0.63}$, where 1 means one merger per present-day remnant. For the other five populations, comparable censuses do not yet exist, so we report upper limits from the best available source constraints, including compact-binary merger rates. Finally, the six adopted upper limit cases yield a present-day gravitational-wave energy budget of $\rho_{\rm gw}^{(6)}=3.33^{+1.32}_{-0.83}\times10^{-7}\rho_c$. A larger energy density would require more source mass, mergers, radiative efficiency, or later epochs, or point to additional source populations or new physics.

一句话导读：该论文基于天体物理源质量与并合率约束，系统计算了六类源引力波背景的基准与能量上限，并指出测量到的振幅无需额外SMBH质量。

相关度：low — 论文研究经典天体物理引力波背景，与用户重点的全息超导、QNM等方向无直接关联。

一个可复现的黑洞-中子星并合画廊示例：面向 Einstein Toolkit

A Reproducible Black Hole-Neutron Star Merger Gallery Example for the Einstein Toolkit

Rahime Matur, Beyhan Karaka\c{s}, Roland Haas, Ian Hawke, Nils Andersson, Steven R. Brandt · astro-ph.HE, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：黑洞-中子星并合是引力波和潜在电磁对应体的重要来源，但公开可用的数值相对论配置仍然有限。本工作展示了一个完全可复现的黑洞-中子星并合模拟，仅使用 Einstein Toolkit 官方 thorn，并针对已探测事件 GW230529 进行配置。我们在三个分辨率下演化系统，最细网格间距分别为 162、222 和 310 米，并评估所得动力学和引力波信号的数值稳健性。从初始数据到参数文件及部分分析脚本全部公开，作为新的 Einstein Toolkit 画廊示例，并随 Hypatia 版本发布，为 Einstein Toolkit 建立参考黑洞-中子星并合配置。

English: Black hole-neutron star mergers are important sources of gravitational waves and potential electromagnetic counterparts, but publicly available numerical relativity configurations for these systems remain limited. In this work, we present a fully reproducible black hole-neutron star merger simulation performed exclusively with official Einstein Toolkit thorns and configured to target the detected event \texttt{GW230529}. We evolve the system at three resolutions, with finest grid spacings of $162$, $222$, and $310$~m, and assess the numerical robustness of the resulting dynamics and gravitational wave signal. The entire setup, from initial data to a parameter file with some of the analysis scripts, is publicly released as a new Einstein Toolkit gallery example and is distributed as part of the Hypatia release, establishing a reference black hole-neutron star merger configuration within the Einstein Toolkit.

一句话导读：发布针对 GW230529 的完全可复现黑洞-中子星并合数值相对论模拟，作为 Einstein Toolkit 新画廊示例。

相关度：high — 论文研究黑洞-中子星并合的经典数值相对论模拟与引力波信号，直接属于经典引力/广义相对论支线。

极质量比旋进在准周期喷发视角下的研究：毫赫兹引力波背景

Extreme Mass Ratio Inspirals in Light of Quasi-periodic Eruptions: Milli-Hertz Gravitational Wave Background

Brennen Black, Xian Chen, Zhen Pan · astro-ph.HE, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-08-27

中文摘要：准周期喷发（QPEs）是源自星系核的重复X射线爆发。在众多模型中，最受青睐的是盘碰撞模型，即一个恒星质量轨道天体与潮汐瓦解事件形成的吸积盘碰撞，每个轨道产生两次耀发。在该模型中，QPEs是环形极质量比旋进（EMRIs）的示踪体，可用于推断EMRI形成率，并估计其对激光干涉空间天线（LISA）频段随机引力波背景（SGWB）的贡献。次天体是恒星质量黑洞还是主序星仍存在争议，这会导致不同的EMRI速率和SGWB结果。我们获得了基准速率——受系统不确定性影响——恒星EMRIs为每星系每年2.88×10^-6，黑洞EMRIs为每星系每年6.07×10^-6，然后估计它们对SGWB的贡献。我们发现只有黑洞EMRIs对LISA可分辨的1-10毫赫兹频带有贡献，取决于次天体质量和形成半径，其贡献可从略低于LISA灵敏度曲线到比其高约两个数量级。恒星EMRIs在到达1-10毫赫兹频带之前就被潮汐瓦解，因此只贡献亚毫赫兹频率，并且低于LISA灵敏度曲线。

English: Quasi-periodic eruptions (QPEs) are repeated X-ray bursts originating in galactic nuclei. Of the many proposed models, the favored model is the disk-collision model in which a stellar mass orbiter collides with a disk formed from a tidal disruption event, generating flares twice per orbit. In this model QPEs are tracers of circular extreme mass ratio inspirals (EMRIs) and can be used to infer the EMRI formation rate and estimate their contribution to the stochastic gravitational wave background (SGWB) in the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) band. Whether the secondary is a stellar-mass black hole or a main sequence star is still debated and leads to different results for the EMRI rate and SGWB. We obtain fiducial rates -- subject to systematic uncertainties -- of $R_{\rm SE} = 2.88\times10^{-6}$ per galaxy per year for stellar EMRIs and $R_{\rm BHE} = 6.07\times10^{-6}$ per galaxy per year for black hole EMRIs, then estimate their contribution to the SGWB. We find that only black hole EMRIs contribute to the 1 - 10 milliHertz band resolvable by LISA, and depending on the secondary mass and formation radius can contribute from just below the LISA sensitivity curve to roughly two orders of magnitude above it. Stellar EMRIs, being tidally disrupted before reaching the 1 - 10 milliHertz band, only contribute to sub-milliHertz frequencies and remain below the LISA sensitivity curve.

一句话导读：利用准周期喷发约束极质量比旋进率，并评估其对LISA频段随机引力波背景的贡献。

相关度：low — 论文涉及经典引力波天文学和EMRI，但没有全息或量子引力内容，与用户关注的全息支线无关。

色-动学对偶与弯曲时空中的双拷贝

Color-Kinematics Duality and the Double Copy in Curved Spacetimes

Mariana Carrillo Gonz\'alez · hep-th, astro-ph.CO, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-08-26

中文摘要：双拷贝将引力数据与规范理论数据联系起来，最直接地体现在平直时空散射振幅的色-动学对偶中。本综述考察了这一思想在渐近平坦背景之外可推广到何种程度。在回顾了用于比较的平直时空基本要素后，我们描述了AdS、dS及其他非平凡背景中边界关联子与散射观测量的弯曲时空构造。接着回顾了经典和场级映射，包括Kerr-Schild、Weyl、Cotton、twistor和卷积双拷贝。另一部分专门讨论自对偶扇区。我们始终强调平直时空中幸存的结构以及曲率引入的障碍。

English: The double copy relates gravitational data to gauge-theory data, most sharply through color-kinematics duality for flat-space scattering amplitudes. This review surveys the extent to which this idea can be formulated beyond asymptotically flat backgrounds. After recalling the flat-space ingredients needed for comparison, we describe curved-space constructions for boundary correlators and scattering observables in AdS, dS, and other non-trivial backgrounds. We then review classical and field-level maps, including Kerr--Schild, Weyl, Cotton, twistor, and convolutional double copies. A separate part is devoted to self-dual sectors. Throughout, we emphasize both the common structures that survive from flat space and the obstructions introduced by curvature.

一句话导读：综述了双拷贝在弯曲时空（包括AdS和dS）中的推广，涵盖边界关联子、经典映射及自对偶扇区。

相关度：low — 本文聚焦弯曲时空中的双拷贝与色-动学对偶，不涉及全息超流、相变或凝聚态等用户重点方向。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

随机矩阵理论的半经典希尔伯特空间

A semiclassical Hilbert space for random matrix theory

Abhirup Bhattacharya, Onkar Parrikar, Vivek Singh · hep-th, math-ph, math.MP · v1 · 更新 2026-08-26

中文摘要：我们研究了随机矩阵理论中热场双态（TFD）的单侧时间演化，其中哈密顿量取自由酉不变厄米矩阵系综抽取的 D×D 随机矩阵。我们论证，在 D→∞ 极限下，以最大纠缠态为初始矢量的 Krylov 基在任意 O(1) 温度和时间内为随机矩阵理论中的这些 TFD 态提供了半经典希尔伯特空间描述，这与双尺度 SYK（DSSYK）模型中的“弦希尔伯特空间”构造非常类似。我们详细研究了谱密度在 D→∞ 极限下为偶函数、紧支撑于一个区间且具有平方根边缘的系综。在对谱密度解析结构的若干额外条件下，我们观察到半经典哈密顿量在较大 Krylov 深度处具有与 DSSYK 相同的渐近行为，相应参数 q=e^{-λ} 与谱密度在谱割之外最近零点的位置相关。此外，在一大类对应于 DSSYK 谱密度紫外（UV）变形的模型中，即在 UV 区修改谱而保持近边缘行为不变的模型中，我们证明 Krylov 基中的半经典有效哈密顿量在低能连续极限下约化为 JT 引力的 Liouville 哈密顿量。这表明我们的半经典希尔伯特空间应被解释为对偶引力描述的体（bulk）希尔伯特空间。

English: We study the one-sided time evolution of thermofield double (TFD) states in random matrix theory, where the Hamiltonian is taken to be a $D\times D$ random matrix drawn from a unitarily invariant emsemble of Hermitian matrices. We argue that the Krylov basis with the maximally entangled state taken as the initial vector gives a semiclassical Hilbert space description of these TFD states in random matrix theory at any $O(1)$ temperature and time in the $D\to \infty$ limit, very analogous to the ``chord Hilbert space'' construction in the double-scaled SYK (DSSYK) model. We study this semiclassical description in detail for ensembles where the spectral density in the $D\to \infty$ limit is even, compactly supported on an interval and has square root edges. With a few more conditions on the analytic structure of the spectral density, we observe that the semiclassical Hamiltonian has the same asymptotic behavior at large Krylov depth as that of DSSYK, with the corresponding parameter $\mathfrak{q}=e^{-\lambda}$ being related to the location of the nearest zero of the spectral density away from the spectral cut. Furthermore, in a large class of models corresponding to ultraviolet deformations of the DSSYK spectral density, i.e., where the spectrum in the UV is modified while leaving the near-edge behavior unchanged, we show that the semiclassical effective Hamiltonian in the Krylov basis reduces to the Liouville Hamiltonian of JT gravity in a low-energy, continuum limit. This suggests that our semiclassical Hilbert space should be interpreted as the bulk Hilbert space of a dual gravity description.

一句话导读：通过 Krylov 基为随机矩阵理论中的 TFD 态构造半经典希尔伯特空间，揭示其与 DSSYK 弦希尔伯特空间及低能极限下 JT 引力 Liouville 哈密顿量的联系，暗示对偶引力体描述。

相关度：low — 本文属于 JT/SYK/AdS2 与随机矩阵理论，未涉及用户关注的任何具体研究支线（全息超流超导、p波、畴壁/孤子、multi-trace、相变/气泡成核、QNM、冷原子、D3-D7/D3-D5探针膜与Floquet驱动）。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

来自 AdS$_2$ 中 Goldstone 玻色子的轫致辐射函数

Bremsstrahlung function from Goldstone bosons in AdS$_2$

Federico Castellani, Lorenzo Di Pietro, Valentina Forini, Roman Stemplowski · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-08-26

中文摘要：我们研究由 N=4 超 Yang-Mills 中半 BPS Wilson 线所对偶的基本弦的 AdS$_2$ 世界面上的量子场论。我们考虑最近提出的围绕 AdS$_2$ 极小曲面的规范固定弦涨落拉格朗日量，并关注非线性实现的 SO(6)/SO(5) 对称性。相关倾斜算符的边界两点函数（其归一化系数正比于轫致辐射函数）可以通过边界算符展开与破缺对称 Noether 流的体两点函数联系起来。我们计算了对流关联函数有贡献的单圈图，讨论了维度正则化的作用，并直接从 AdS$_2$ 世界面量子场论恢复了已知的轫致辐射函数的强耦合修正。

English: We study quantum field theory on the AdS$_2$ worldsheet of the fundamental string dual to the half-BPS Wilson line of $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills. We consider a recently proposed gauge-fixed string fluctuation Lagrangian around the AdS$_2$ minimal surface, and focus on the nonlinearly realized $SO(6)/SO(5)$ symmetry. The boundary two-point function of the associated tilt operators, whose normalization coefficient is proportional the Bremsstrahlung function, can be related to the bulk two-point function of the broken-symmetry Noether current through the boundary operator expansion. We evaluate the one-loop diagrams contributing to the current correlator, discussing the role of dimensional regularization and recovering the known strong-coupling correction to the Bremsstrahlung function directly from AdS$_2$ worldsheet quantum field theory.

一句话导读：利用 AdS$_2$ 世界面量子场论和 Goldstone 玻色子方法，从破缺对称 Noether 流相关函数计算中恢复了 Wilson 线轫致辐射函数的已知强耦合修正。

相关度：medium — 论文涉及全息 Wilson 线对应、AdS2 世界面量子场论与对称性破缺，属于 AdS/CFT 字典和强耦合计算方向，但与用户给出的冷凝态、非平衡、D-brane 驱动等重点标签无直接重叠，因此相关性中等。

固定荷下的全息扩展复杂度：Routhian、膜与弦

Holographic Spread Complexity at Fixed Charge: Routhians, Branes and Strings

Dimitrios Chatzis, Madison Hammond, Carlos Nunez, Alfonso V. Ramallo, Ricardo T. Santamaria · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-08-26

中文摘要：全息扩展（Krylov）复杂度将边界态复杂度的增长与落入体态的探针的径向动量联系起来。幺正演化使扩展复杂度成为时间的偶函数。我们证明，当探针携带守恒的 Noether 荷并由未约化的拉格朗日量描述时，这一要求会失效。解决办法简单而普遍：转入固定荷扇区的 Routhian，即可恢复复杂度在短时间行为的正确形式。我们从第一性原理确立了这一处理方式，并在大量探针族中加以检验：带电粒子、张力和电荷失谐的非 BPS D-膜、沿内部等距方向激发的膜、世界体积规范场、AdS4×CP3 中的涨落 D0-膜，以及 AdS5×S5 中绕转与转动结合的基本弦；此外还补充了 AdS3×S3×T4、ABJM 和带电荷的 Anabalón-Ross 背景等例子。我们将这些结果转化为 Krylov 链数据，提取 Lanczos 系数和 Krylov 数关联子，并提出带荷、延展探针的复杂度自然地组织为集体贡献、涨落贡献、电荷贡献和混合贡献。这种分解为构建真正场论化、多种子全息复杂度开辟了一条具体路径。

English: Holographic spread (Krylov) complexity relates the growth of a boundary state's complexity to the proper radial momentum of a probe falling into the bulk. Unitary evolution makes spread complexity an even function of time. We show that this requirement fails whenever a probe carries a conserved Noether charge and is described by its unreduced Lagrangian. The cure is simple and universal: passing to the Routhian of the fixed-charge sector restores the correct short-time behaviour of the complexity. We establish this prescription from first principles and test it across an extensive family of probes: charged particles, non-BPS D-branes with detuned tension and charge, branes excited along internal isometries, worldvolume gauge fields, a fluctuating D0-brane in AdS$_4\times \mathbb{CP}^3$ and fundamental strings combining winding with rotation in AdS$_5\times \mathrm{S}^5$ complemented by further examples in AdS$_3\times \mathrm{S}^3\times T^4$, ABJM, and the charged Anabal\'on-Ross background. We then translate these results into Krylov-chain data, extracting Lanczos coefficients and Krylov-number correlators, and propose that complexity for charged, extended probes organises naturally into collective, fluctuation, charge and mixed contributions. This decomposition opens a concrete path toward a genuinely field-theoretic, multi-seed construction of holographic complexity.

一句话导读：带守恒荷的全息扩展复杂度需用固定荷扇区 Routhian 恢复偶时间行为，并在多种膜/弦探针中验证和映射到 Krylov 链数据。

相关度：low — 论文属于全息复杂度与 AdS/CFT 量子信息方向，与用户指定的重点标签（全息超流超导、QNM、相变、D3-D7 探针膜等）无直接重叠，因此相关性低。

全息得到的Hagedorn温度：旋转和带点系统

Hagedorn temperature from holography: rotating and charged systems

Francesco Bigazzi, Tommaso Canneti, Federico Castellani, Aldo L. Cotrone · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-08-26

中文摘要：利用弦论/场论对应作为工具，我们研究了强耦合平面规范理论的Hagedorn温度对角速度和U(1)整体流化学势的依赖。我们的结果来源于世界面半经典量子化方法与靶空间低能有效方法的相互作用。Hagedorn温度以逆（大）'t Hooft耦合极限的展开形式给出。在涨落的二次阶近似下，世界面分析提供了领头阶、次领头阶以及部分次次领头阶项。特别地，它捕获了由于非零模式对零点能的贡献而产生的NNLO log2项。这些模式不被有效方法所考虑，在有效方法中必须通过适当的经验猜测手动加入。另一方面，有效方法允许相对容易地计算缺失的NNLO部分（甚至进一步的次领头修正），这些修正只有通过在世界面方法中处理涨落的四次（或更高）阶才能得到。我们给出了普遍结果以及不同维度下的几个例子，包括限制模型和球面上的共形场论（如N=4 SYM和ABJM），它们对偶于全局AdS空间上的弦论。

English: Using the string/field theory correspondence as a tool, we study the dependence of the Hagedorn temperature of strongly coupled, planar gauge theories on angular velocities and chemical potentials for $U(1)$ global currents. Our results are obtained from an interplay of world-sheet semiclassical quantization methods and target space low energy effective ones. The Hagedorn temperature is given as an expansion in the inverse (large) 't Hooft coupling limit. Working to quadratic order in the fluctuations, the world-sheet analysis provides the leading, the next-to-leading and part of the next-to-next-to leading order terms. In particular, it captures the NNLO $\log 2$ terms which are due to the contributions of non-zero modes to the zero-point energy. These modes are not accounted for by the effective approach, within which they have to be included by hand using suitable educated guesses. On the other hand, the effective methods allow to compute in a relatively easy way the missing NNLO pieces (and even further subleading corrections), which could be accounted for from the world-sheet approach only working at quartic (or higher) order in the fluctuations. We present general results and several examples in various dimensions, including both confining models and conformal field theories on spheres (such as $\mathcal N=4$ SYM and ABJM) dual to string theories on global $AdS$ spaces.

一句话导读：通过全息对应研究旋转和带电荷的强耦合规范理论的Hagedorn温度，结合世界面与有效方法给出高阶修正。

相关度：high — 论文直接使用全息对应（弦论/场论对应）研究强耦合规范理论的Hagedorn温度，属于AdS/CFT基础字典范畴。
